# Bend voxel rewrite

Run the editable Light Atelier sculpture court in a native window. The default scene contains Suzanne, Oculus, Twist, a backdrop, and a slatted canopy. Use `--cube` to open the original 8³ body.

![Light Atelier sculpture court](docs/atelier.png)

## Vulkan renderer

The native Vulkan renderer lives in [vulkan/](vulkan/README.md), alongside the
existing Bend-native renderer. It uses the same voxel world and reference modules.

```sh
git submodule update --init --recursive deps/bend-vulkan
make vulkan
make vulkan-run
```

Use `make vulkan-proof` for its laws and `make vulkan-verify` for its real-runtime
acceptance suite. Vulkan rendering needs Bend 2.0.39, Clang, Vulkan headers/driver,
and X11 for the interactive window; it does not require CUDA.

## Run the slice

Use upstream [Bend 2.0.39](https://github.com/bendlang/bend/releases/tag/v2.0.39). Run `bend update` to install the latest release and `bend version` to check the installed version. Native GPU builds require the compiler and SDK for the available Bend execution lane. On Linux, install `libx11-dev` for the window.

```sh
	make
	make run
```

`make run` uses CPU rendering with Bend's default worker count, the logical CPU count. The native Atelier workload ran faster on CPU than GPU on the development machine. Override the execution lane and worker count through `RUN_ARGS`:

```sh
	make run RUN_ARGS='+RTS --gpu on -RTS'
	make run RUN_ARGS='+RTS --gpu off --threads 4 -RTS'
```

Bend 2.0.39 reads runtime options between `+RTS` and `-RTS`. Put application options such as `--cube` and `--dump` outside that block.

Use these controls:

- Hold **W/S** to fly along the view direction, and **A/D** to strafe.
- Hold **Q/E** to descend or ascend along the world's vertical axis.
- Hold the right mouse button and move the mouse to look around.
- Press **Space** to carve the Oculus edge in the opening view, or the front corner with `--cube`.
- Move the mouse over the body to preview a yellow sphere and its selected cells. Click to carve at the continuous surface point; while holding RMB, the brush targets the screen centre.
- Press **R** to restore the scene while keeping your camera position and view.
- Press **Escape**, or close the window, to exit.

The perspective camera can fly through the scene. Movement uses elapsed time and normalizes combined directions. Each voxel is 10cm wide. The spherical brush has a 20cm radius and removes occupied cells whose centres are inside or on its boundary. With `--cube`, Space uses the fixed centre (65,65,65)cm; repeating that cut leaves the body unchanged. Hovering over the background clears the preview.

The scene uses green foundation at material ID 1, tan concrete at ID 2, blue frame at ID 3, orange machinery at ID 4, and pale plaster at ID 5. Colors come from the [stable OKLCH material palette](https://github.com/aivv73/bend-voxel/commit/02bbd4a6ab4ca746333473f6ca8b7ace9513ae61). Daylight follows the [reference lighting change](https://github.com/aivv73/bend-voxel/commit/cf188d2afeaf47836ffe023907f81efafc40878c): warm directional sunlight, cool sky ambient light, and soft sun shadows on voxel surfaces and the ground. Shading runs in linear RGB before sRGB encoding. The sun-depth cache survives camera movement and rebuilds after edits. Night mode and the camera work light are omitted. Carving preserves each surviving cell's material, and appearance depends on material, face direction, and sun visibility. Unsupported positive material IDs remain occupied and display diagnostic magenta.

Engine callers can fill or paint the existing body with named materials:

```bend
	import ./material.bend as M
	import ./voxel.bend as V

	body = V.Body.filled(3n, M.Material.id(M.Concrete{}))
	body = V.Body.write(body, V.Cell{2, 3, 4}, M.Material.id(M.Machinery{}))
```

To run on the CPU, disable GPU dispatch through Bend's runtime option:

```sh
	./build/bend-voxel-rewrite +RTS --gpu off -RTS
```

To require the available GPU lane, force GPU execution:

```sh
	./build/bend-voxel-rewrite +RTS --gpu on -RTS
```

Keep `build/bend-voxel-rewrite.gpu` beside the executable. On the development Linux machine, the CUDA SDK is at `/opt/cuda`. The Makefile detects that path at the build boundary. To use a different SDK path, set `CUDA_HOME`:

```sh
	make CUDA_HOME=/path/to/cuda
```

## Verify the slice

Run the formal laws, geometry reference cases, and complete CPU/GPU image comparisons:

```sh
	make test
```

Validation uses formal laws, actual engine geometry, real renderer exports, and native UI evidence. First-party validation includes no traditional test suites. The geometry reference check is written in Bend and compares complete cell and face sets, including materials. Run it separately with `make geometry`. `make lighting` compares every sun-depth texel against an occupied-cell ray reference, checks daylight ramps and self-shadow prevention, validates every compressed illumination sample, verifies camera cache reuse, and compares opposite-view CPU/GPU exports. `make materials` checks the palette's runtime properties and exports all four materials on CPU and GPU under `build/materials`. `make images` forces both `+RTS --gpu off -RTS` and `+RTS --gpu on -RTS` across four tile and fork configurations. Native UI checks cover flight, brush targeting, capture, and lifecycle behavior.

To compare images on a machine without a GPU, run the CPU cases:

```sh
	make build/image-checker
	./build/image-checker +RTS --gpu off -RTS --cpu-only
```

The checker reserves a fresh directory under `build/image-check` for each renderer dispatch and reads its new `render.ppm`. A zero-exit renderer that writes no file fails. Every complete 512x512 export must match the fixed full-scene or cut-scene RGB SHA-256 golden. Exact CPU variant and CPU/GPU comparisons run separately from the golden check. The sample pixels and 9,779-pixel destruction delta remain required. Read [the golden update procedure](docs/verification.md#update-the-image-goldens) before changing expected hashes.

The checker resolves the repository from its executable path, so absolute or relative invocations also work from another directory. If you invoke a bare executable name through `PATH`, supply `--root /path/to/repository`. Use `--binary path/to/renderer` to select another renderer. Relative renderer paths resolve under that root.

The parser accepts the renderer's unsigned ASCII P3 format with literal `P3 512 512 255` header tokens and exactly 786,432 channels in 0..255. ASCII whitespace and leading zeros in channels are accepted. PPM comments, signed values, digit underscores, and Unicode digits are rejected.

The native window checks use upstream Bend 2.0.39 and pinned BendHub packages for [window references](https://hub.bend-lang.com/0x3d4f2512cce00e5099d03d9e1b3b9ae1) and [window capture](https://hub.bend-lang.com/0x493e83fed22415cb35e84da752fefdef). Input and lifecycle checks use [bend-ui-test](https://github.com/aivv73/bend-ui-test), pinned as the `deps/bend-ui-test` submodule. Initialize the dependency and run:

```sh
	git submodule update --init --recursive
	make ui
	./build/ui-check +RTS --gpu off -RTS --output .audit/screenshots/cpu
	./build/ui-check +RTS --gpu on -RTS --close-method window --output .audit/screenshots/window-close
```

The GPU target saves native PPM screenshots in `.audit/screenshots/ui`. The CPU command saves them in `.audit/screenshots/cpu`.

The native check uses the slice's `Interactive.step`, which handles pointer capture and delegates the real view and event reducer to `App.step`. `Window.capture` reads the owned window's client pixels after earlier output completes. Keep the client fully on-screen and unobscured during the test. `scripts/ui_capture.bend` converts its RGB array to an `Image`; Bend checks the pixels and exports screenshots. The checker passes `WindowRef` directly to `UITest.key`, `UITest.click`, `UITest.request_close`, and `UITest.alive`. The library owns native event delivery and expiry checks. A close request goes through the app reducer, which closes the owned window. The check requires Linux/X11 and `libX11`. Unsupported backends report an error.

To verify actual held flight keys and captured mouse motion, put `xdotool` on `PATH` and run `make flight-ui`. This separate Bend check uses the same `Interactive.step` and saves native captures in `.audit/screenshots/flight-ui`. `make brush-ui` checks actual mouse hover, background clearing, complete removal sets for free and captured clicks, and same-hover resets. It saves captures in `.audit/screenshots/brush-ui`. Run native tests sequentially.

The packages' native effects use Bend runtime internals. After updating Bend, run `make -B test` and `make -B ui` to rebuild and validate the engine and window bridges. After checking out a different project revision, run `git submodule update --init --recursive` to select its pinned library version. Read [the verification notes](docs/verification.md) for the scope of formal proofs and runtime checks.

## Export an image

Write the geometry view to a deterministic PPM file. These exports omit the interactive hover preview:

```sh
	./build/bend-voxel-rewrite +RTS --gpu on -RTS --dump build/full.ppm
	./build/bend-voxel-rewrite +RTS --gpu on -RTS --cut --dump build/destroyed.ppm
```

## Measure native frames

Measure displayed frames with cached scenes and scripted destruction and reset:

```sh
	make benchmark
```

The Bend command records sequential CPU and GPU measurements in `build/benchmark.json`. Each child opens a native window and excludes eight warm-up frames. CPU thread counts and GPU work decomposition are measured separately. The default CPU counts are one, half the logical system processors, and all logical system processors, with duplicate defaults removed. Override the workload and CPU counts through the built benchmark:

```sh
	./build/benchmark +RTS --gpu off -RTS --frames 120 --repeats 3 --cpu-threads 1 4 8
```

Samples measure elapsed integer milliseconds divided by measured frames, in ms/frame, with 53-bit binary precision. The median selects the middle sample for an odd count and averages the two middle samples for an even count. Progress rounds the stored sample to exactly three fractional digits with ties to even. Report numbers retain the full stored precision. Repeated thread entries run repeated measurements and share one report row. Each alternate repeat reverses the complete configuration list.

Numeric arguments accept unsigned ASCII decimal integers, including leading zeros. Frames must be in 24..4294967295, repeats in 2..4294967295, and thread counts in 1..4294967295. Options accept `--flag=value`; later occurrences replace earlier values. `--cpu-threads` accepts one or more counts. Use `--output path/to/report.json` to select a report path relative to your current directory.

The benchmark resolves its repository from the canonical executable or script path, including a bare executable found through `PATH`. `--root /path/to/repository` selects another root. `--binary path/to/renderer` accepts an absolute path or a path under that root. Child processes run in the repository directory. The host helpers require POSIX `sh`, `getconf`, and GNU `realpath`, `dirname`, `date`, and `mkdir`; the current GNU/Linux host is verified. Read [the measurement contract and limits](docs/verification.md#native-measurement).

To choose a work decomposition, set both depths:

```sh
	./build/bend-voxel-rewrite +RTS --gpu on -RTS --tile-depth 6 --fork-depth 6 --bench 120
```

The default tile and fork depths are both six. Native daylight measurements selected that configuration for the available CPU and GPU lanes.

`--tile-depth` chooses the tile tree depth within the 512-pixel image. `--fork-depth` chooses how many tile tree levels may fork. Both default to six and accept values from zero to nine. Leaves run sequentially. These settings change scheduling and candidate lists while preserving the pixels.

For native frame attribution and steady cached FPS, build the phase driver:

```sh
	make build/frame-profile
	./build/frame-profile +RTS --gpu off -RTS --frames 12
	./build/frame-profile +RTS --gpu off -RTS --bench 24
```

`--frames` reports initialization stages, cached rendering, hover preview, flight, cut, and reset. It separates model updates, rendering, presentation, and image disposal. Initialization is reconstructed for attribution, so use its stage times as diagnostics. `--bench` measures cached frames in a native window after eight warm-up frames, with initialization excluded. It keeps the camera fixed and performs no edits. The production renderer's `--bench` includes cut and reset. Both consume and dispose of every rendered image.

Read [the design notes](docs/design.md) for the data flow, boundaries, and examples considered.

## Compare a minimal DDA renderer

Build the independent voxel-traversal renderer and compare every image ray with the existing renderer:

```sh
	make dda
	./build/dda-compare +RTS --gpu off --threads 1 -RTS --verify
	./build/dda-compare +RTS --gpu off --threads 1 -RTS --verify-steps
	./build/dda-compare +RTS --gpu off -RTS --renderer dda --dump build/dda.ppm
	make build/dda-benchmark
	./build/dda-benchmark +RTS --gpu off --threads 12 -RTS --frames 12 --repeats 5 --label cpu-12 --output build/dda-comparison.tsv
```

The comparison includes the production renderer and a version of the existing face renderer with shadows omitted to match minimal DDA shading. Read [the DDA comparison](docs/dda-comparison.md) for scope, validation, images, and measurements. The default DDA crosses compressed uniform regions. [DDA region traversal](docs/dda-optimization.md) compares the upstream world representations and explains the optimization. [The region DDA profile](docs/dda-profile.md) records cumulative CPU costs, actual work counters, and one rejected search optimization. [Corrected event counting](docs/dda-estimate.md) replaces that baseline's binary search with a bounded estimate and exact plane correction, with repeated CPU12 gains and separate GPU measurements. Add `--steps` to the benchmark to include the unit-step reference.

[Per-ray subtree caching](docs/dda-cursor.md) records a rejected optimization, its Bend prototype, exact correctness checks, and complete-frame measurements. Fewer tree visits did not offset the added ownership work; the retained DDA is unchanged.

[Explicit zero-event guards](docs/dda-zero.md) records four checked short-circuiting prototypes. Broader guards regress on GPU; the smallest avoids advancement plane calculations but adds a repeated CPU cost. The retained DDA is unchanged.

[Redundant floor removal](docs/dda-floor.md) simplifies the retained event estimate. Exact surfaces and image bytes are preserved. GPU fork-seven measurements improve in two batches; parallel CPU samples favor the baseline.

[Boundary event counts](docs/dda-boundary.md) reuse the known integer limit for axes leaving a region. Exact surfaces and image bytes are preserved. CPU1, CPU6, and CPU12 improve in repeated complete-window measurements. GPU gains remain inconclusive.

[Indexed compressed-tree storage](docs/dda-indexed.md) records a checked paged-index prototype and its rejected complete-frame measurements. The accepted DDA remains installed.

[GPU decomposition and pixel assignment](docs/dda-gpu.md) adds a typed Bend sweep and an opt-in interleaved layout. It improves the measured large-scene GPU workload in two batches. CPU and carved-cube results retain contiguous fork six as the default.

### vulkan/shaders/appearance_abi.frag
```
#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require
#include "appearance_frame.glsl"
#include "appearance_words.glsl"
layout(location=0) out vec4 outputColor;
void main() {
  uint word = appearance_frame_word(uint(gl_FragCoord.y) * 512u + uint(gl_FragCoord.x));
  outputColor = vec4(uvec4(word,word>>8u,word>>16u,word>>24u) & uvec4(255u)) / 255.0;
}
```

### vulkan/shaders/appearance_backdrop.frag
```
#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require
#include "appearance_common.glsl"
#include "appearance_pcf.glsl"
layout(location=0) out vec4 outputColor;
void main() {
  uint canonicalY = uint(floor(gl_FragCoord.y * 512.0 / frame.viewport.y));
  uint packed = 1054752u + (min(canonicalY, 511u) / 32u) * 65793u;
  vec3 display = vec3(uvec3(packed >> 16u, packed >> 8u, packed) & uvec3(255u)) / 255.0;
  vec3 linear = appearance_decode(display);
  vec3 ray = appearance_ray(gl_FragCoord.xy);
  if (frame.eye_near.z > -0.5 && ray.z < 0.0) {
    float t = (-0.5 - frame.eye_near.z) / ray.z;
    vec3 point = frame.eye_near.xyz + t * ray;
    if (all(greaterThanEqual(point.xy, vec2(-16))) && all(lessThanEqual(point.xy, vec2(24)))) {
      vec2 f = fract(point.xy * 0.5);
      bool grid = any(lessThan(f, vec2(0.0125))) || any(greaterThan(f, vec2(0.9875)));
      vec3 base = grid ? vec3(0.055, 0.065, 0.08) : vec3(0.10, 0.12, 0.15);
      linear = appearance_day(base, vec3(0,0,1), appearance_visibility(point, vec3(0,0,1)));
    }
  }
  outputColor = appearance_finish(linear);
}
```

### vulkan/shaders/appearance_backdrop.vert
```
#version 450
void main() {
  const vec2 corners[6] = vec2[6](vec2(-1,-1), vec2(1,-1), vec2(1,1), vec2(1,1), vec2(-1,1), vec2(-1,-1));
  gl_Position = vec4(corners[gl_VertexIndex], 0, 1);
}
```

### vulkan/shaders/appearance_common.glsl
```
#ifndef APPEARANCE_COMMON
#define APPEARANCE_COMMON
#include "appearance_frame.glsl"

vec3 appearance_decode(vec3 display) {
  return mix(pow((display + 0.055) / 1.055, vec3(2.4)), display / 12.92,
             lessThanEqual(display, vec3(0.04045)));
}
vec3 appearance_encode(vec3 linear) {
  vec3 c = clamp(linear, 0.0, 1.0);
  return mix(1.055 * pow(c, vec3(1.0 / 2.4)) - 0.055, 12.92 * c,
             lessThanEqual(c, vec3(0.0031308)));
}
vec4 appearance_finish(vec3 linear) {
  return vec4(frame.flags.y != 0u ? clamp(linear, 0.0, 1.0) : appearance_encode(linear), 1.0);
}
vec4 appearance_raw(vec4 bytes) {
  return vec4(frame.flags.y != 0u ? appearance_decode(bytes.rgb) : bytes.rgb, bytes.a);
}
vec3 appearance_ray(vec2 pixel) {
  precise vec2 offset = vec2(pixel.x - frame.viewport.x * 0.5,
                             frame.viewport.y * 0.5 - pixel.y);
  precise vec3 horizontal = frame.camera_right.xyz * (offset.x / frame.camera_forward_focal.w);
  precise vec3 vertical = frame.camera_up.xyz * (offset.y / frame.camera_forward_focal.w);
  precise vec3 tangent = horizontal + vertical;
  precise vec3 ray = frame.camera_forward_focal.xyz + tangent;
  return ray;
}
vec3 appearance_normal(uint d) {
  if (d < 2u) return vec3(d == 0u ? -1.0 : 1.0, 0, 0);
  if (d < 4u) return vec3(0, d == 2u ? -1.0 : 1.0, 0);
  return vec3(0, 0, d == 4u ? -1.0 : 1.0);
}
vec3 appearance_axes(vec3 p, uint d) {
  return d < 2u ? p.xyz : d < 4u ? p.yxz : p.zxy;
}
bool appearance_intersection(vec3 anchor, vec2 extent, uint d, vec3 ray,
                             out precise float t, out precise float u, out precise float v) {
  precise vec3 eye = appearance_axes(frame.eye_near.xyz, d);
  precise vec3 origin = appearance_axes(anchor, d);
  precise vec3 delta = appearance_axes(ray, d);
  precise float plane = origin.x + ((d & 1u) != 0u ? 1.0 : 0.0);
  precise float distance = plane - eye.x;
  precise float uEye = eye.y - origin.y;
  precise float vEye = eye.z - origin.z;
  if (delta.x == 0.0) return false;
  t = distance / delta.x;
  precise float uNumerator = uEye + t * delta.y;
  precise float vNumerator = vEye + t * delta.z;
  u = uNumerator / extent.x;
  v = vNumerator / extent.y;
  if (isnan(t) || isinf(t) || t < frame.eye_near.w) return false;
  return true;
}
bool appearance_cell(vec3 anchor, vec2 extent, uint d, vec3 ray,
                     out uvec3 cell, out vec3 point) {
  precise float t;
  precise float u;
  precise float v;
  if (!appearance_intersection(anchor, extent, d, ray, t, u, v)) return false;
  precise float localU = u * extent.x;
  precise float localV = v * extent.y;
  uvec2 offset = uvec2(clamp(floor(vec2(localU, localV)), vec2(0.0), extent - 1.0));
  cell = uvec3(anchor);
  if (d < 2u) cell.yz += offset;
  else if (d < 4u) cell.xz += offset;
  else cell.xy += offset;
  precise vec3 step = ray * t;
  point = frame.eye_near.xyz + step;
  return true;
}
bool appearance_selected(uvec3 cell) {
  if (frame.flags.x == 0u) return false;
  for (uint i = 0u; i < frame.flags.w; ++i) {
    if (all(equal(cell, frame.selected[i].xyz))) return true;
  }
  return false;
}
vec3 appearance_day(vec3 base, vec3 normal, float visibility) {
  vec3 sun = normalize(vec3(-0.45, 0.35, 0.82));
  float sky = normal.z < -0.5 ? 0.0 : normal.z > 0.5 ? 1.0 : 0.5;
  float direct = max(dot(normal, sun), 0.0) * visibility;
  return base * (vec3(0.17, 0.20, 0.27) + sky * vec3(0.23, 0.24, 0.21)
                 + direct * vec3(0.88, 0.76, 0.61));
}
#endif
```

### vulkan/shaders/appearance_frame.glsl
```
layout(std140, set=0, binding=0) uniform AppearanceFrame {
  mat4 camera_clip;
  mat4 light_clip;
  vec4 eye_near;
  vec4 camera_right;
  vec4 camera_up;
  vec4 camera_forward_focal;
  vec4 viewport;
  vec4 brush_cm;
  uvec4 flags;
  vec4 palette[5];
  vec4 shadow_params;
  uvec4 selected[125];
} frame;
```

### vulkan/shaders/appearance_pcf.glsl
```
layout(set=1, binding=0) uniform sampler2D appearance_shadow_map;
float appearance_visibility(vec3 point, vec3 normal) {
  if (dot(normal, normalize(vec3(-0.45, 0.35, 0.82))) <= 0.0) return 1.0;
  float resolution = frame.shadow_params.x;
  float span = frame.shadow_params.y;
  float inverseDepth = frame.shadow_params.z;
  vec3 right = vec3(frame.light_clip[0][0], frame.light_clip[1][0], frame.light_clip[2][0]) * (span * 0.5);
  vec3 up = vec3(frame.light_clip[0][1], frame.light_clip[1][1], frame.light_clip[2][1]) * (-span * 0.5);
  vec3 forward = vec3(frame.light_clip[0][2], frame.light_clip[1][2], frame.light_clip[2][2]) / inverseDepth;
  vec3 localNormal = vec3(dot(normal, right), dot(normal, up), dot(normal, forward));
  float nz = localNormal.z < 0.0 ? -max(abs(localNormal.z), 1e-6) : max(abs(localNormal.z), 1e-6);
  vec2 slope = vec2(-localNormal.x / nz, localNormal.y / nz) * (span / resolution);
  vec3 clip = (frame.light_clip * vec4(point, 1.0)).xyz;
  if (clip.z < 0.0 || clip.z > 1.0) return 1.0;
  vec2 uv = (clip.xy * 0.5 + 0.5) * resolution;
  ivec2 base = ivec2(floor(uv));
  float lit = 0.0;
  for (int y = -1; y <= 1; ++y) {
    for (int x = -1; x <= 1; ++x) {
      ivec2 tap = base + ivec2(x, y);
      if (any(lessThan(tap, ivec2(0))) || any(greaterThanEqual(tap, ivec2(resolution)))) {
        lit += 1.0;
      } else {
        vec2 delta = vec2(tap) + 0.5 - uv;
        float receiver = clip.z + (dot(slope, delta) - frame.shadow_params.w) * inverseDepth;
        float stored = textureLod(appearance_shadow_map, (vec2(tap) + 0.5) / resolution, 0.0).r;
        lit += receiver <= stored ? 1.0 : 0.0;
      }
    }
  }
  return lit / 9.0;
}
```

### vulkan/shaders/appearance_ring.frag
```
#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require
#include "appearance_common.glsl"
layout(location=0) flat in vec4 endpoints;
layout(location=1) flat in vec2 inverseDepth;
layout(location=2) flat in uint visible;
layout(location=0) out vec4 outputColor;
void main() {
  if (visible == 0u) discard;
  vec2 delta = endpoints.zw - endpoints.xy;
  float squared = dot(delta,delta);
  float t = squared > 0.0 ? clamp(dot(gl_FragCoord.xy - endpoints.xy, delta) / squared, 0.0, 1.0) : 0.0;
  vec2 error = gl_FragCoord.xy - endpoints.xy - t * delta;
  if (dot(error,error) > 0.5625) discard;
  float inverse = inverseDepth.x + t * (inverseDepth.y - inverseDepth.x);
  if (inverse <= 0.0) discard;
  float depth = 1.0 / inverse;
  if (depth < frame.eye_near.w) discard;
  float biasedDepth = max(frame.eye_near.w, depth - 0.0001);
  gl_FragDepth = 1.0 - frame.eye_near.w / biasedDepth;
  outputColor = appearance_finish(vec3(1,1,0));
}
```

### vulkan/shaders/appearance_ring.vert
```
#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require
#include "appearance_frame.glsl"
layout(location=0) flat out vec4 endpoints;
layout(location=1) flat out vec2 inverseDepth;
layout(location=2) flat out uint visible;
vec3 ringPoint(uint ring, uint index) {
  float angle = float(index) * 0.1963495408;
  float c = frame.brush_cm.w / 10.0 * cos(angle);
  float s = frame.brush_cm.w / 10.0 * sin(angle);
  vec3 delta = ring == 0u ? vec3(c,s,0) : ring == 1u ? vec3(c,0,s) : vec3(0,c,s);
  return frame.brush_cm.xyz / 10.0 + delta;
}
vec3 eyePoint(vec3 p) {
  vec3 d = p - frame.eye_near.xyz;
  return vec3(dot(d, frame.camera_right.xyz), dot(d, frame.camera_up.xyz), dot(d, frame.camera_forward_focal.xyz));
}
vec3 nearCross(vec3 a, vec3 b, float near) {
  vec3 low = a.z < b.z ? a : b;
  vec3 high = a.z < b.z ? b : a;
  float t = (near - low.z) / (high.z - low.z);
  return vec3(low.xy + t * (high.xy - low.xy), near);
}
void main() {
  uint segment = uint(gl_VertexIndex) / 6u;
  uint corner = uint(gl_VertexIndex) % 6u;
  uint ring = segment / 32u;
  uint index = segment % 32u;
  vec3 a = eyePoint(ringPoint(ring, index));
  vec3 b = eyePoint(ringPoint(ring, index + 1u));
  float near = frame.eye_near.w;
  visible = frame.flags.x != 0u && (a.z >= near || b.z >= near) ? 1u : 0u;
  endpoints = vec4(0);
  inverseDepth = vec2(1);
  if (visible == 0u) { gl_Position = vec4(0,0,0,1); return; }
  if (a.z < near) a = nearCross(a, b, near);
  else if (b.z < near) b = nearCross(a, b, near);
  vec2 p = frame.viewport.xy * 0.5 + frame.camera_forward_focal.w * vec2(a.x, -a.y) / a.z;
  vec2 q = frame.viewport.xy * 0.5 + frame.camera_forward_focal.w * vec2(b.x, -b.y) / b.z;
  endpoints = vec4(p,q);
  inverseDepth = 1.0 / vec2(a.z,b.z);
  vec2 delta = q - p;
  float squared = dot(delta,delta);
  vec2 tangent = squared > 0.0 ? delta / sqrt(squared) : vec2(1,0);
  vec2 normal = vec2(-tangent.y,tangent.x);
  bool end = corner == 1u || corner == 2u || corner == 3u;
  bool plus = corner == 2u || corner == 3u || corner == 4u;
  vec2 pixel = (end ? q : p) + (end ? 0.75 : -0.75) * tangent + (plus ? 0.75 : -0.75) * normal;
  gl_Position = vec4(pixel / frame.viewport.xy * 2.0 - 1.0, 0.0, 1.0);
}
```

### vulkan/shaders/appearance_words.glsl
```
uint appearance_frame_word(uint i) {
  if (i >= 0u && i < 16u) {
    uint j = i - 0u;
    return floatBitsToUint(frame.camera_clip[j / 4u][j % 4u]);
  }
  if (i >= 16u && i < 32u) {
    uint j = i - 16u;
    return floatBitsToUint(frame.light_clip[j / 4u][j % 4u]);
  }
  if (i >= 32u && i < 36u) {
    uint j = i - 32u;
    return floatBitsToUint(frame.eye_near[j]);
  }
  if (i >= 36u && i < 40u) {
    uint j = i - 36u;
    return floatBitsToUint(frame.camera_right[j]);
  }
  if (i >= 40u && i < 44u) {
    uint j = i - 40u;
    return floatBitsToUint(frame.camera_up[j]);
  }
  if (i >= 44u && i < 48u) {
    uint j = i - 44u;
    return floatBitsToUint(frame.camera_forward_focal[j]);
  }
  if (i >= 48u && i < 52u) {
    uint j = i - 48u;
    return floatBitsToUint(frame.viewport[j]);
  }
  if (i >= 52u && i < 56u) {
    uint j = i - 52u;
    return floatBitsToUint(frame.brush_cm[j]);
  }
  if (i >= 56u && i < 60u) {
    uint j = i - 56u;
    return frame.flags[j];
  }
  if (i >= 60u && i < 80u) {
    uint j = i - 60u;
    return floatBitsToUint(frame.palette[j / 4u][j % 4u]);
  }
  if (i >= 80u && i < 84u) {
    uint j = i - 80u;
    return floatBitsToUint(frame.shadow_params[j]);
  }
  if (i >= 84u && i < 584u) {
    uint j = i - 84u;
    return frame.selected[j / 4u][j % 4u];
  }
  return 0u;
}
```

### vulkan/shaders/appearance_world.frag
```
#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require
#include "appearance_common.glsl"
#include "appearance_pcf.glsl"
layout(location=0) flat in vec3 legacyColor;
layout(location=1) flat in uvec4 material;
layout(location=2) flat in uint direction;
layout(location=3) flat in vec3 anchor;
layout(location=4) flat in vec2 extent;
layout(location=0) out vec4 outputColor;
vec4 integerBytes(uint value) {
  return vec4(uvec4(value, value >> 8u, value >> 16u, value >> 24u) & uvec4(255u)) / 255.0;
}
void main() {
  if (frame.flags.z == 1u) { outputColor = appearance_raw(vec4(legacyColor, 1.0)); return; }
  if (frame.flags.z == 2u) { outputColor = appearance_raw(vec4(material) / 255.0); return; }
  if (frame.flags.z == 3u) { outputColor = appearance_raw(vec4(float(direction) / 255.0, 0, 0, 1)); return; }
  uvec3 cell;
  vec3 point;
  if (!appearance_cell(anchor, extent, direction, appearance_ray(gl_FragCoord.xy), cell, point)) discard;
  if (frame.flags.z >= 4u && frame.flags.z <= 6u) {
    outputColor = appearance_raw(integerBytes(cell[frame.flags.z - 4u])); return;
  }
  if (frame.flags.z == 7u) {
    outputColor = appearance_raw(vec4(appearance_selected(cell) ? 1.0 : 0.0, 0, 0, 1)); return;
  }
  uint id = material.x | (material.y << 8u) | (material.z << 16u) | (material.w << 24u);
  vec3 normal = appearance_normal(direction);
  vec3 lit = vec3(1, 0, 1);
  if (id >= 1u && id <= 5u) {
    lit = appearance_day(frame.palette[id - 1u].rgb, normal, appearance_visibility(point, normal));
  }
  if (appearance_selected(cell)) lit = vec3(1, 1, 0);
  outputColor = appearance_finish(lit);
}
```
