### vulkan/tools/check_measurement.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import argparse
from collections import deque
from contextlib import nullcontext
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory
from types import SimpleNamespace
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch


sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def make_run(side='candidate', workload='edits', pair=0):
    return subject.Run(f'{side}-{workload}-{pair}', side, 'test', workload, pair,
                       ['/native'], ['/native'], 'binary-hash', '/unused/log', {}, {})


def state(frame=10, revision=0, **extra):
    return dict(rows=32, frame=frame, generation=1, revision=revision,
                installed=revision, uploads=2 + 2 * revision, quads=100,
                width=640, height=480, mapped=1, captured=0,
                camera='1,2,3,4,5', **extra)


def line(values):
    return 'STATE ' + ' '.join(f'{key}={value}' for key, value in values.items()) + '\n'


class FakeInput:
    def button(self, *_):
        pass

    def key(self, *_):
        pass


class ScriptedTrace:
    def __init__(self, run, batches):
        self.batches = deque(batches)
        self.stream = io.BytesIO()
        self.trace = subject.Trace(run, self.stream)

    def poll(self):
        if self.batches:
            position = self.stream.tell()
            self.stream.seek(0, io.SEEK_END)
            self.stream.write(''.join(line(s) for s in self.batches.popleft()).encode())
            self.stream.seek(position)
        return self.trace.poll()


def edit_driver(batches, side='candidate', provenance=None):
    run = make_run(side)
    run.legacy_completion_provenance = provenance
    trace = ScriptedTrace(run, [[state()], *batches])
    previous = trace.poll()
    process = SimpleNamespace(poll=lambda: None)
    args = SimpleNamespace(timeout=0.05, poll_ms=0.01)
    driver = subject.WindowRun(run, FakeInput(), process, trace, SCRATCH, args)
    return driver, run, previous


def static_image(label, values, rgb_hash):
    return dict(label=label, state=dict(values=values), attempts=[dict(
        passed=True, nonblank=True,
        signature=dict(width=640, height=480, rgb_sha256=rgb_hash))])


def paired_runs(workload='edits', pairs=1):
    runs = []
    for pair in range(pairs):
        for side in ('baseline', 'candidate'):
            run = make_run(side, workload, pair)
            run.ended_utc = '2026-10-17T00:00:00+00:00'
            if workload == 'flight':
                run.metrics['completed_frames_per_second'] = 20
                run.images = [static_image('flight-initial', state(), 'intact'),
                              static_image('flight-final', state(frame=50), side)]
            else:
                run.metrics.update(cut_observed_latency_ms=dict(median=10),
                                   reset_observed_latency_ms=dict(median=20))
                run.images = [static_image('edit-original', state(), 'intact')]
                for action, revision, quads, rgb in [('cut', 1, 90, 'cut'),
                                                      ('reset', 2, 100, 'intact')]:
                    values = state(frame=10 + revision, revision=revision)
                    values['quads'] = quads
                    run.images.append(static_image(f'edit-00-{action}', values, rgb))
                    run.edit_latencies.append(dict(action=action, cycle=0,
                                                   completed=True, warmup=False))
            runs.append(run)
    return runs


class CompletionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_stale_untagged_overlap_cannot_complete(self):
        driver, run, before = edit_driver([[state(frame=10, revision=1)],
                                          [state(frame=11, revision=1)]])
        with self.assertRaises(TimeoutError):
            driver.edit('cut', before, 0, False)
        self.assertEqual(run.edit_latencies[0]['completed'], False)

    def test_first_installed_tagged_record_is_completion(self):
        driver, run, before = edit_driver([
            [state(frame=11, revision=1, completed_revision=1, presented_revision=1)],
            [state(frame=12, revision=1, completed_revision=1, presented_revision=1)]])
        end = driver.edit('cut', before, 0, False)
        self.assertEqual(end['line'], 2)
        self.assertEqual(run.edit_latencies[0]['completed'], True)
        self.assertEqual(run.edit_latencies[0]['completed_frame_increment'], 1)

    def test_buffered_first_tagged_completion_is_not_lost(self):
        driver, _, before = edit_driver([
            [state(frame=11, revision=1, completed_revision=1),
             state(frame=12, revision=1, completed_revision=2)],
            [state(frame=13, revision=1, completed_revision=2)]])
        self.assertEqual(driver.edit('cut', before, 0, False)['line'], 2)

    def test_completion_needs_frame_advance_from_before_edit(self):
        driver, run, before = edit_driver([
            [state(frame=10, revision=1, completed_revision=1)],
            [state(frame=11, revision=1, completed_revision=1)]])
        self.assertEqual(driver.edit('cut', before, 0, False)['values']['frame'], 11)
        self.assertEqual(run.edit_latencies[0]['completed'], True)

    def test_first_completion_keeps_raw_receipt_and_dispatch_timestamps(self):
        driver, run, before = edit_driver([
            [state(frame=11, revision=1, completed_revision=1)],
            [state(frame=12, revision=1, completed_revision=1)]])
        run.elapsed_ns = iter((1_000_000_000, 1_000_000_100,
                               1_000_000_200, 1_250_000_000,
                               1_300_000_000, 1_500_000_000)).__next__
        end = driver.edit('cut', before, 0, False)
        self.assertEqual(end['observed_ns'], 1_250_000_000)
        self.assertEqual(run.edit_latencies[0]['input_start_ns'], 1_000_000_000)
        self.assertEqual(run.edit_latencies[0]['input_dispatched_ns'], 1_000_000_100)
        self.assertEqual(run.edit_latencies[0]['observed_latency_ms'], 250.0)

    def test_future_and_sentinel_tags_do_not_complete(self):
        for tag in (2, 4294967295, 18446744073709551615):
            for name in ('completed_revision', 'presented_revision'):
                with self.subTest(tag=tag, name=name):
                    driver, run, before = edit_driver([
                        [state(frame=10, revision=1)],
                        [state(frame=11, revision=1, **{name: tag})]])
                    with self.assertRaises(TimeoutError):
                        driver.edit('cut', before, 0, False)
                    self.assertEqual(run.edit_latencies[0]['completed'], False)

    def test_conflicting_completion_tags_do_not_complete(self):
        driver, _, before = edit_driver([
            [state(frame=10, revision=1)],
            [state(frame=11, revision=1, completed_revision=1,
                   presented_revision=4294967295)]])
        with self.assertRaises(TimeoutError):
            driver.edit('cut', before, 0, False)

    def test_legacy_provenance_does_not_allow_candidate_fallback(self):
        driver, _, before = edit_driver([[state(frame=10, revision=1)],
                                        [state(frame=11, revision=1)]],
                                       provenance=dict(frames_in_flight=1))
        with self.assertRaises(TimeoutError):
            driver.edit('cut', before, 0, False)

    def test_unverified_baseline_cannot_use_untagged_fallback(self):
        driver, _, before = edit_driver([[state(frame=10, revision=1)],
                                        [state(frame=11, revision=1)]], side='baseline')
        with self.assertRaises(TimeoutError):
            driver.edit('cut', before, 0, False)

    def test_verified_single_frame_baseline_has_separate_fallback(self):
        provenance = dict(binary_sha256='93a9ef7f43e457323d08d2754d55e4e60fb97eb4ee7b1ced0a4fd4c0f9e52ec2',
                          source_commit='928bee9e677983c063811e0540f8783d86467a83',
                          frames_in_flight=1)
        driver, run, before = edit_driver([[state(frame=10, revision=1)],
                                          [state(frame=11, revision=1)]],
                                         side='baseline', provenance=provenance)
        self.assertEqual(driver.edit('cut', before, 0, False)['values']['frame'], 11)
        self.assertEqual(run.edit_latencies[0]['completed'], True)

    def test_verified_baseline_cannot_fall_back_from_future_tags(self):
        driver, _, before = edit_driver([
            [state(frame=10, revision=1)],
            [state(frame=11, revision=1, completed_revision=4294967295)]],
            side='baseline', provenance=dict(frames_in_flight=1))
        with self.assertRaises(TimeoutError):
            driver.edit('cut', before, 0, False)


class PairTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_matching_static_pairs_are_eligible(self):
        runs = paired_runs(pairs=5)
        result = subject.summarize(runs, 5)[0]
        self.assertEqual(result['successful_pairs'], 5)
        self.assertEqual(len(result['paired_samples']), 5)
        self.assertEqual(result['verdict'], 'inconclusive')

    def test_wrong_static_images_fail_before_metrics(self):
        for label in ('edit-original', 'edit-00-cut', 'edit-00-reset'):
            with self.subTest(label=label):
                runs = paired_runs(pairs=5)
                image = next(i for i in runs[1].images if i['label'] == label)
                image['attempts'][-1]['signature']['rgb_sha256'] = 'wrong-scene-or-cut'
                result = subject.summarize(runs, 5)[0]
                self.assertEqual(result['successful_pairs'], 4)
                self.assertEqual(len(result['paired_samples']), 4)
                self.assertEqual(result['failure_count'], 2)
                self.assertEqual(result['failed_runs'], 2)
                self.assertEqual(runs[0].errors[0]['kind'], 'pair_comparison')

    def test_wrong_static_geometry_or_view_fails(self):
        for label in ('edit-original', 'edit-00-cut', 'edit-00-reset'):
            for field, value in [('quads', 123), ('camera', '6,7,8,9,10'),
                                 ('width', 800), ('height', 600),
                                 ('mapped', 0), ('captured', 1)]:
                with self.subTest(label=label, field=field):
                    runs = paired_runs()
                    image = next(i for i in runs[1].images if i['label'] == label)
                    image['state']['values'][field] = value
                    result = subject.summarize(runs, 1, smoke=True)[0]
                    self.assertEqual(result['successful_pairs'], 0)
                    self.assertEqual(result['failure_count'], 2)

    def test_missing_static_capture_fails(self):
        runs = paired_runs()
        runs[1].images.pop()
        result = subject.summarize(runs, 1, smoke=True)[0]
        self.assertEqual(result['successful_pairs'], 0)
        self.assertEqual(result['failure_count'], 2)

    def test_time_driven_flight_final_need_not_match(self):
        runs = paired_runs('flight')
        runs[1].images[-1]['state']['values']['camera'] = '6,7,8,9,10'
        result = subject.summarize(runs, 1, smoke=True)[0]
        self.assertEqual(result['successful_pairs'], 1)
        self.assertEqual(result['failure_count'], 0)

    def test_flight_initial_static_mismatch_fails(self):
        runs = paired_runs('flight')
        runs[1].images[0]['attempts'][-1]['signature']['rgb_sha256'] = 'wrong-scene'
        result = subject.summarize(runs, 1, smoke=True)[0]
        self.assertEqual(result['successful_pairs'], 0)
        self.assertEqual(result['failure_count'], 2)

    def test_report_serializes_pair_failures_and_does_not_duplicate_them(self):
        runs = paired_runs()
        runs[1].images[1]['attempts'][-1]['signature']['rgb_sha256'] = 'wrong-cut'
        report = dict(mode='smoke', errors=[])
        path = SCRATCH / 'report.json'
        for _ in range(2):
            subject.write_report(path, report, runs, 1)
        actual = json.loads(path.read_text())
        self.assertEqual(actual['failure_count'], 2)
        self.assertEqual([run['passed'] for run in actual['runs']], [False, False])
        self.assertEqual(actual['summary'][0]['successful_pairs'], 0)

    def test_partial_pair_is_not_eligible(self):
        result = subject.summarize(paired_runs()[:1], 1, smoke=True)[0]
        self.assertEqual(result['successful_pairs'], 0)
        self.assertEqual(result['sides']['baseline']['cut_observed_latency_ms'], None)


class ProvenanceTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_main_enables_legacy_only_for_known_manifest_commit_and_baseline_side(self):
        for case in ('verified', 'wrong-hash', 'wrong-commit', 'missing-manifest',
                     'missing-commit', 'alternate-path'):
            with self.subTest(case=case):
                directory = SCRATCH / f'provenance-{case}'
                directory.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
                binary = directory / 'baseline-native'
                binary.write_bytes(b'Not a native executable. Never executed.\n')
                binary.chmod(0o700)
                sha256 = hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                commit = 'known-single-frame-fixture'
                trusted = dict(binary_sha256='different-hash' if case == 'wrong-hash' else sha256,
                               source_commit=commit)
                if case != 'missing-manifest':
                    (directory / 'milestone-7-baseline-window.sha256').write_text(sha256 + '\n')
                if case != 'missing-commit':
                    (directory / 'milestone-7-baseline-commit.txt').write_text(
                        'different-commit' if case == 'wrong-commit' else commit)
                captured = []

                def execute(run, *_):
                    captured.append(run)
                    run.errors.append(dict(kind='fixture', message='No native execution'))
                    run.ended_utc = '2026-10-17T00:00:00+00:00'
                    return run

                with (patch.object(subject, 'BUILD', directory),
                      patch.object(subject, 'BASELINE', directory / 'other-native'
                                   if case == 'alternate-path' else binary),
                      patch.object(subject, 'SINGLE_FRAME_BASELINE', trusted, create=True),
                      patch.object(subject, 'isolated_display', lambda _: nullcontext()),
                      patch.object(subject, 'execute_run', execute),
                      patch.object(subject.shutil, 'which', return_value=sys.executable),
                      patch.object(subject.subprocess, 'run', return_value=SimpleNamespace(
                          returncode=0, stdout='', stderr='')),
                      patch.object(subject.os, 'sched_getaffinity', return_value={0}, create=True),
                      patch.object(subject.os, 'getloadavg', return_value=(0.0, 0.0, 0.0), create=True),
                      patch.object(sys, 'stdout', io.StringIO())):
                    result = subject.main(['--baseline', str(binary), '--candidate', str(binary),
                                           '--smoke', '--workload', 'flight',
                                           '--output', str(directory / 'report.json')])
                self.assertEqual(result, 1)
                self.assertEqual(len(captured), 2)
                provenance = getattr(captured[0], 'legacy_completion_provenance', None)
                self.assertEqual(provenance is not None, case == 'verified')
                if case == 'verified' and provenance is not None:
                    self.assertEqual(provenance['frames_in_flight'], 1)
                    self.assertEqual(provenance['binary_sha256'], sha256)
                self.assertIsNone(getattr(captured[1], 'legacy_completion_provenance', None))


class ResourceAndStageTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_missing_stage_data_remains_unavailable(self):
        result = subject.stage_timings(make_run())
        self.assertEqual({name: data['available'] for name, data in result.items()},
                         dict(cpu_meshing=False, upload=False, gpu_render=False,
                              presentation=False))
        self.assertEqual(result['gpu_render']['timestamp_capabilities']['available'], False)

    def test_resource_report_does_not_claim_unproven_plateau(self):
        run = make_run()
        initial = dict(values=state())
        args = SimpleNamespace(warmup_edits=1, edits=4, row_tolerance=8)
        driver = subject.WindowRun(run, FakeInput(), None, None, SCRATCH, args)
        driver.capture = lambda *_args, **_kwargs: 'image-signature'
        driver.settle = lambda record: record

        def edit(action, previous, cycle, warmup):
            values = state(frame=previous['values']['frame'] + 1,
                           revision=previous['values']['revision'] + 1)
            values['quads'] = 90 if action == 'cut' else 100
            values['rows'] = [32, 32, 34, 33, 34][cycle]
            run.edit_latencies.append(dict(action=action, completed=True, warmup=warmup,
                                           observed_latency_ms=10))
            return dict(values=values)

        driver.edit = edit
        driver.checkpoint = lambda label, _: run.resource_samples.append(
            dict(source='checkpoint', label=label, native_counts=None))
        driver.edits(initial)
        self.assertEqual(run.metrics['cut_observed_latency_ms']['count'], 4)
        descriptions = ' '.join(a['description'] for a in run.assertions)
        self.assertNotIn('stable plateau', descriptions)
        self.assertTrue(any('counters were absent' in text for text in run.measurement_limits))


class StageProtocolTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_exact_cpu_limbs_and_labels(self):
        run = make_run()
        run.diagnostics = [dict(observed_ns=7, line='producer', values=dict(
            cpu_scope='upload_prepare', ns_lo=0xffffffff, ns_hi=0xffffffff))]
        output = subject.stage_timings(run)['upload']['fields']['cpu_scope.upload_prepare_ns']
        self.assertEqual(output['samples'][0]['value'], (1 << 64) - 1)
        self.assertEqual(output['producer_scope'], 'upload_prepare')

    def test_finite_gpu_bits_and_capability(self):
        run = make_run()
        run.diagnostics = [dict(observed_ns=7, line='producer', values=dict(
            gpu_scope='shadow', ns_f32_bits=0x41800000,
            timestamp_valid_bits=36, timestamp_period_ns_f32_bits=0x3f800000,
            timestamp_supported=1))]
        output = subject.stage_timings(run)['gpu_render']
        self.assertEqual(output['fields']['gpu_scope.shadow_ns']['samples'][0]['value'], 16.0)
        self.assertEqual(output['timestamp_capabilities']['fields']['timestamp_period_ns'][0]['value'], 1.0)

    def test_invalid_protocol_values_are_unavailable(self):
        for values in (dict(cpu_scope='upload_prepare', ns_lo=-1, ns_hi=0),
                       dict(cpu_scope='upload_prepare', ns_lo=True, ns_hi=0),
                       dict(gpu_scope='main', ns_f32_bits=0x7f800000),
                       dict(gpu_scope='main', ns_f32_bits=0x7fc00000),
                       dict(gpu_scope='main', ns_f32_bits=0xbf800000)):
            run = make_run()
            run.diagnostics = [dict(observed_ns=7, line='producer', values=values)]
            self.assertFalse(any(row['available'] for row in subject.stage_timings(run).values()))


def main(argv=None):
    global subject, SCRATCH
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description='Check measurement-driver regression behavior without native execution.')
    parser.add_argument('module', nargs='?', type=Path, default=ROOT / 'tools/measure_m7.py',
                        help='measurement driver to check (default: sibling measure_m7.py)')
    options, unittest_args = parser.parse_known_args(argv)
    module = options.module.resolve()
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'tools'))
    sys.path.insert(0, str(module.parent))
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('measured_subject', module)
    subject = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[spec.name] = subject
    with TemporaryDirectory(prefix='measurement-check-') as directory:
        SCRATCH = Path(directory)
        spec.loader.exec_module(subject)
        result = unittest.main(argv=[sys.argv[0], *unittest_args], exit=False).result
        return 0 if result.wasSuccessful() else 1


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
```

### vulkan/tools/check_presentation.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run controlled presentation failure probes beside actual X11 Vulkan execution."""
import argparse
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess
from check_window import isolated_display, instrument_window, deduplicate_native

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BUILD=ROOT/'build'

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--sanitize',action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--isolated-display',action='store_true')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    BUILD.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    source=BUILD/'presentation-probes.c';binary=BUILD/'presentation-probes'
    subprocess.run(['bend','tests/presentation_probe_main.bend','-o',str(source)],cwd=ROOT,check=True)
    emitted=deduplicate_native(source.read_text())
    common=(ROOT/'../deps/bend-vulkan/tests/probes.c').read_text()
    allocator=common.split('static unsigned bv_test_barrier_calls;')[0]
    result_check=common[common.index('static void bv_test_result('):common.index('static Term bv_test_regions(')]
    probes=result_check+(ROOT/'tests/presentation_probes.c').read_text()
    macros=re.findall(r'^#define (CID_\w+) \d+$',emitted,re.M)
    def cid(match):
        name=match[1].replace('.','_')
        matches=[m for m in macros if m=='CID_'+name or m.endswith('_Vulkan_'+name)]
        if len(matches)!=1: raise ValueError(f'Missing or ambiguous constructor {name}: {matches}')
        return matches[0]
    probes=re.sub(r'CID\(([^)]+)\)',cid,probes)
    begin=emitted.index('// Bend native effect ABI.')
    end=emitted.index('static void __attribute__((constructor)) bv_register(void)',begin)
    native=emitted[begin:end]
    native=native.replace('malloc(','bv_test_alloc(').replace('calloc(','bv_test_calloc(').replace('realloc(','bv_test_realloc(')
    native=re.sub(r'\bfree\(','bv_test_free(',native)
    native=native.replace('static BvRow* bv_add(',allocator+'\nstatic BvRow* bv_add(',1)
    a=native.index('static Term bv_live_objects(');b=native.index('\n}',a)
    native=native[:a]+native[a:b].replace('return io_done(e,count);','if(!count)assert(bv_test_live_allocations==0);return io_done(e,count);')+native[b:]
    emitted=emitted[:begin]+native+probes+'\n'+emitted[end:]
    for name in ('create_swapchain','acquire','present','u64_roundtrip','create_graphics_pipeline'):
        emitted,n=re.subn(r'(io_eff\(CID_\w+, )bv_'+name+r'(\);)',r'\1test_'+name+r'\2',emitted)
        if n!=1: raise ValueError(f'{name} registration moved')
    source.write_text(instrument_window(emitted))
    flags=['-fsanitize=address,undefined','-fno-omit-frame-pointer'] if args.sanitize else []
    subprocess.run(['clang','-std=c11','-O1','-g',*flags,str(source),'-lpthread','-lm','-ldl','-o',str(binary)],cwd=ROOT,check=True)
    env=dict(os.environ,VK_INSTANCE_LAYERS='VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation',VK_LAYER_VALIDATE_SYNC='1',ASAN_OPTIONS='detect_leaks=0',UBSAN_OPTIONS='halt_on_error=1')
    with isolated_display(args.isolated_display):
        env['DISPLAY']=os.environ['DISPLAY']
        completed=subprocess.run([str(binary)],cwd=ROOT,env=env,text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=60)
    text=completed.stdout+completed.stderr
    (BUILD/'presentation-probes.log').write_text(text)
    print(text,end='',flush=True)
    completed.check_returncode()
    for marker in ('Controlled swapchain changing enumeration','Controlled acquire lifecycle','Independent acquisition completion','Same-index reacquisition binds and submits','Controlled present OOM retry','Native presentation borrowing','Bend target pipeline failure'):
        if marker not in text: raise AssertionError(f'Missing executed probe group {marker}')
    if 'Validation Error' in text or 'SYNC-HAZARD' in text: raise AssertionError(text)

if __name__=='__main__': main()
```

### vulkan/tools/check_production.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess
import sys

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BUILD=ROOT/'build/milestone-6-production'
COMPILE_TIMEOUT=1800
FORBIDDEN=('Validation Error','SYNC-HAZARD','ERROR: AddressSanitizer','runtime error:','SOME PROOFS FAIL')


def producer_sources():
    paths=[ROOT/'../deps/bend-vulkan/Vulkan.bend',ROOT/'tests/appearance_render.bend',ROOT/'tools/build_shaders.py']
    paths += list(ROOT.parent.glob('*.bend'))
    paths += [p for folder in ('examples', 'shaders', 'src', '../deps/bend-vulkan/src',
                              '../deps/bend-vulkan/examples', '../deps/bend-vulkan/shaders',
                              '../deps/bend-vulkan/deps/bend-collections/src')
              for p in sorted((ROOT/folder).rglob('*'))
              if p.is_file() and p.suffix in ('.bend','.vert','.frag','.glsl','.c','.h')]
    return {os.path.relpath(p,ROOT):hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in paths}


def shadow_gate():
    output=BUILD/'shadow-causality'
    snapshot=output/'snapshot'
    snapshot.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    device=subprocess.run(['vulkaninfo'],cwd=ROOT,text=True,capture_output=True,check=True,timeout=60)
    device_path=output/'device.txt'
    device_path.write_text(device.stdout)
    (BUILD/'device-precision.log').write_text(device.stdout)
    (output/'device.stderr').write_text(device.stderr)
    sources=[BUILD/(state+suffix) for state in ('intact','carved')
             for suffix in ('.body.json','.uniform','.shadow')]
    sources += [ROOT/'build/milestone-6-reference/receivers.jsonl',device_path]
    before={p:p.stat() for p in sources}
    inputs=[]
    for path in sources:
        data=path.read_bytes()
        (snapshot/path.name).write_bytes(data)
        inputs.append(dict(name=path.name,source=str(path),size=len(data),
                           mtime_ns=before[path].st_mtime_ns,sha256=hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()))
    for path in sources:
        after=path.stat()
        assert (before[path].st_ino,before[path].st_size,before[path].st_mtime_ns)==(after.st_ino,after.st_size,after.st_mtime_ns), 'Capture changed during shadow snapshot'
        assert hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()==next(r['sha256'] for r in inputs if r['source']==str(path))
    (snapshot/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(inputs=inputs),indent=2)+'\n')
    provenance=ROOT/'build/milestone-6-reference/dependency-sha256.json'
    (snapshot/'reference-dependency-sha256.json').write_bytes(provenance.read_bytes())
    commands=[]
    controls={}
    gate_sources={name:hashlib.sha256((ROOT/name).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                  for name in ('tools/check_production.py','tools/shadow_causality.py')}
    for control in (None,'clear_missing_pass','zero_depth','shifted_one_texel','stable_depth_spike','edge_depth_spike'):
        name=control or 'actual'
        target=output/'negative-controls'/name if control else output
        command=[sys.executable,'tools/shadow_causality.py','--snapshot',str(snapshot),
                 '--device',str(snapshot/'device.txt'),'--output',str(target)]
        if control:command+=['--negative-control',control]
        result=subprocess.run(command,cwd=ROOT,text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=600)
        (output/(name+'.log')).write_text(result.stdout+result.stderr)
        commands.append(dict(command=command,status=result.returncode,expected_status=1 if control else 0,
                             log=name+'.log',gate_sources=gate_sources))
        (output/'commands.json').write_text(json.dumps(commands,indent=2)+'\n')
        assert result.returncode==(1 if control else 0), (name,result.stdout+result.stderr)
        report=json.loads((target/'results.json').read_text())
        assert report['pass_gate']==(control is None), name
        if control:
            assert all(r['unexplained_depth_count']>0 for r in report['states'].values()), name
            controls[control]=dict(status=result.returncode,pass_gate=report['pass_gate'],
                                   unexplained_depth_counts={s:r['unexplained_depth_count'] for s,r in report['states'].items()})
    report=json.loads((output/'results.json').read_text())
    report['negative_controls']=controls
    report['negative_controls_pass']=len(controls)==5
    report['gate_sources']=gate_sources
    (output/'results.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\n')
    for row in inputs:
        assert hashlib.sha256((snapshot/row['name']).read_bytes()).hexdigest()==row['sha256']
    print('Original-Body actual512 D32 caster/visibility causality and five corrupt-depth negative controls passed')
    print('Shadow evidence: '+str(output/'results.json'))


def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--sanitize',action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--shadow-only',action='store_true',help='check coherent existing captures without rendering again')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    BUILD.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    if args.shadow_only:
        shadow_gate()
        return
    sources=producer_sources()
    (BUILD/'producer-source-sha256.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(timing='before Bend emission',sources=sources),indent=2)+'\n')
    commands=[]

    def run(command,name,env=None,timeout=600):
        command=list(map(str,command))
        result=subprocess.run(command,cwd=ROOT,env=env,text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=timeout)
        (BUILD/(name+'.log')).write_text(result.stdout+result.stderr)
        commands.append(dict(command=command,status=result.returncode,log=name))
        (BUILD/'commands.json').write_text(json.dumps(commands,indent=2)+'\n')
        assert result.returncode==0 and not any(value in result.stdout+result.stderr for value in FORBIDDEN),name
        return result.stdout

    source=BUILD/'production.c'; binary=BUILD/'production'
    run(['bend','tests/appearance_render.bend','-o',source],'bend-c',timeout=COMPILE_TIMEOUT)
    run(['clang','-std=c11','-O1',source,'-lpthread','-lm','-ldl','-o',binary],'compile',timeout=COMPILE_TIMEOUT)
    preprocessed=BUILD/'production-preprocessed.c'
    run(['clang','-std=c11','-E','-P',source,'-o',preprocessed],'preprocess')
    assert not re.search(r'\b(?:cuLaunchKernel|cuInit|nvrtcCreateProgram)\s*\(',preprocessed.read_text()), 'Headless production retains CUDA dispatch'
    linked=run(['ldd',binary],'linked')
    assert not any(value in linked for value in ('libX11','libxcb')), 'Headless production gained a display dependency'
    device=run(['vulkaninfo','--summary'],'device')
    run(['vulkaninfo'],'device-precision')
    validation='VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation' in device
    normal_output=run([binary,'+RTS','--threads','4','-RTS'],'normal')
    assert 'Production shared recorder live=0' in normal_output
    run([sys.executable,'tools/production_oracle.py'],'normal-oracle')
    (BUILD/'normal-results.json').write_bytes((BUILD/'results.json').read_bytes())

    def artifact_hashes():
        return {p.name:hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in sorted(BUILD.iterdir())
                if p.suffix in ('.rgba','.depth','.shadow','.uniform') or p.name.endswith('.body.json')}

    expected=artifact_hashes()
    statuses=dict(normal='pass',validation='unavailable',sanitized='not requested')
    validation_env=dict(os.environ,VK_INSTANCE_LAYERS='VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation',VK_LAYER_VALIDATE_SYNC='1') if validation else dict(os.environ)
    if validation:
        output=run([binary,'+RTS','--threads','4','-RTS'],'validation',validation_env)
        assert 'Production shared recorder live=0' in output
        assert expected==artifact_hashes(), 'Repeated validation run changed exact production artifacts'
        run([sys.executable,'tools/production_oracle.py'],'validation-oracle')
        (BUILD/'validation-results.json').write_bytes((BUILD/'results.json').read_bytes())
        statuses['validation']='pass'
    if args.sanitize:
        sanitized=BUILD/'production-sanitized'
        run(['clang','-std=c11','-O1','-g','-fsanitize=address,undefined','-fno-omit-frame-pointer',source,
             '-lpthread','-lm','-ldl','-o',sanitized],'sanitizer-compile',timeout=COMPILE_TIMEOUT)
        output=run([sanitized,'+RTS','--threads','4','-RTS'],'sanitized',dict(validation_env,ASAN_OPTIONS='detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1',UBSAN_OPTIONS='halt_on_error=1'))
        assert 'Production shared recorder live=0' in output
        assert expected==artifact_hashes(), 'Sanitizers changed exact production artifacts'
        run([sys.executable,'tools/production_oracle.py'],'sanitized-oracle')
        (BUILD/'sanitized-results.json').write_bytes((BUILD/'results.json').read_bytes())
        statuses['sanitized']='pass'
    run([sys.executable,'tools/reference_lighting.py','--gpu-metadata',BUILD/'gpu-receivers.json',
         '--gpu-depth',BUILD/'intact.shadow'],'actual-gpu-depth-oracle')
    shadow_gate()
    assert sources==producer_sources(), 'Renderer source changed during production acceptance'
    producer=dict(sources=sources,emitted_c_sha256=hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
                  normal_binary_sha256=hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest())
    if args.sanitize:producer['sanitized_binary_sha256']=hashlib.sha256(sanitized.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    (BUILD/'source-artifact-sha256.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(producer=producer,artifacts=expected,statuses=statuses),indent=2)+'\n')
    print('Production exact raw/shaded coverage, original identity, actual512 D32 PCF, palette, transfer, rings and repeated frame acceptance passed')
    print(json.dumps(statuses,indent=2))


if __name__=='__main__':main()
```

### vulkan/tools/check_reference_lighting.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BUILD = ROOT/'build/milestone-6-reference'


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--verify-existing', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    BUILD.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    commands = []

    def run(command, name):
        result = subprocess.run(list(map(str, command)), cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=600)
        (BUILD/name).write_text(result.stdout)
        (BUILD/(name+'.stderr')).write_text(result.stderr)
        commands.append(dict(command=list(map(str, command)), status=result.returncode, log=name))
        (BUILD/('verify-existing-commands.json' if args.verify_existing else 'commands.json')).write_text(json.dumps(commands, indent=2)+'\n')
        assert result.returncode == 0 and 'SOME PROOFS FAIL' not in result.stdout+result.stderr, name
        return result.stdout

    if not args.verify_existing:
        for source, name, output in [('reference_resolution','resolution-probe','resolution-runtime.log'),
                                     ('reference_matrix','matrix-dump','matrix-palette.jsonl'),
                                     ('reference_lighting','evidence','receivers.jsonl')]:
            run(['bend', f'tests/{source}.bend', '-o', BUILD/name], name+'-compile.log')
            run([BUILD/name, '+RTS', '--threads', '4', '-RTS'], output)
    assert (BUILD/'resolution-runtime.log').read_text().strip() == 'base_width=128 effective_atelier_width=512'
    run([sys.executable, 'tools/reference_lighting.py'], 'verification.log')
    hashes = {p.name: hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in (ROOT/'..').glob('*.bend')}
    (BUILD/'dependency-sha256.json').write_text(json.dumps(hashes, indent=2)+'\n')
    print('Original Body/Surface cached512/direct512, explicit128 experiment, 2226 receivers and exact 16-cell cut/repeat/reset passed')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### vulkan/tools/check_retirement.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import argparse
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--sanitize', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    flags = ['-fsanitize=address,undefined', '-fno-omit-frame-pointer'] if args.sanitize else []
    environment = dict(os.environ, VK_INSTANCE_LAYERS='VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation', VK_LAYER_VALIDATE_SYNC='1',
                       ASAN_OPTIONS='detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1', UBSAN_OPTIONS='halt_on_error=1')
    for name, markers in (
        ('generation_recording', ('Index-only Recording publication rejection preserved all old bytes/handles/counters/rows',
                                  'Mixed reused/changed/removed generation retained exact resident handles')),
        ('legacy_retirement', ('Legacy complete upload and empty replacement retirement rejection preserved old bytes',)),
    ):
        source = ROOT / f'build/m7-{name}.c'
        binary = source.with_suffix('')
        subprocess.run(['bend', f'tests/{name}.bend', '-o', str(source)], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
        subprocess.run(['clang', '-std=c11', '-O1', '-g', *flags, str(source), '-lpthread', '-lm', '-ldl', '-o', str(binary)], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
        result = subprocess.run([str(binary)], cwd=ROOT, env=environment, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=60)
        output = result.stdout + result.stderr
        (ROOT / f'build/m7-{name}.log').write_text(output)
        print(output, end='', flush=True)
        result.check_returncode()
        assert all(marker in output for marker in markers)
        assert not any(item in output for item in ('Validation Error', 'SYNC-HAZARD', 'AddressSanitizer', 'runtime error:'))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### vulkan/tools/check_timing.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import argparse
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--sanitize', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    source = ROOT / 'build/m7-timing-reset.c'
    source.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    binary = source.with_suffix('')
    subprocess.run(['bend', 'tests/timing_reset.bend', '-o', str(source)], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
    emitted = source.read_text()
    probes = (ROOT / 'tests/timing_reset_probes.c').read_text()
    anchor = 'static void __attribute__((constructor)) bv_register(void)'
    assert emitted.count(anchor) == 1
    emitted = emitted.replace(anchor, probes + '\n' + anchor)
    allocator = (ROOT / '../deps/bend-vulkan/tests/probes.c').read_text().split('static unsigned bv_test_barrier_calls;')[0]
    begin = emitted.index('// Bend native effect ABI.')
    end = emitted.index(anchor)
    native = emitted[begin:end]
    native = native.replace('malloc(', 'bv_test_alloc(').replace('calloc(', 'bv_test_calloc(').replace('realloc(', 'bv_test_realloc(')
    native = re.sub(r'\bfree\(', 'bv_test_free(', native)
    native = native.replace('static BvRow* bv_add(', allocator + '\nstatic BvRow* bv_add(', 1)
    emitted = emitted[:begin] + native + emitted[end:]
    macros = re.findall(r'^#define (CID_\w+) \d+$', emitted, re.M)
    matches = [m for m in macros if m == 'CID_Fail']
    assert len(matches) == 1
    emitted = emitted.replace('CID(Fail)', matches[0])
    emitted, count = re.subn(r'(io_eff\(CID_\w+, )bv_reset_timestamp_queries(\);)', r'\1test_timing_reset\2', emitted)
    assert count == 1
    source.write_text(emitted)
    flags = ['-fsanitize=address,undefined', '-fno-omit-frame-pointer'] if args.sanitize else []
    subprocess.run(['clang', '-std=c11', '-O1', '-g', *flags, str(source), '-lpthread', '-lm', '-ldl', '-o', str(binary)], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
    environment = dict(os.environ, VK_INSTANCE_LAYERS='VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation', VK_LAYER_VALIDATE_SYNC='1',
                       ASAN_OPTIONS='detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1', UBSAN_OPTIONS='halt_on_error=1')
    result = subprocess.run([str(binary)], cwd=ROOT, env=environment, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=60)
    output = result.stdout + result.stderr
    (ROOT / 'build/m7-timing-reset.log').write_text(output)
    print(output, end='', flush=True)
    result.check_returncode()
    assert 'Actual query reset recorded-ref allocation failed with old completed data retained' in output
    assert 'TIMING unavailable=gpu_frame_queries_disabled' in output
    assert 'Completed old queries plus reset-ref OOM disabled frame diagnostics and recovered on retry' in output
    assert 'gpu_scope=' not in output
    assert not any(item in output for item in ('Validation Error', 'SYNC-HAZARD', 'AddressSanitizer', 'runtime error:'))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### vulkan/tools/check_uploads.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import argparse
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--sanitize', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    source = ROOT / 'build/m7-upload-failures.c'
    binary = source.with_suffix('')
    subprocess.run(['bend', 'tests/upload_failures.bend', '-o', str(source)], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
    emitted = source.read_text()
    probes = (ROOT / 'tests/upload_failure_probes.c').read_text()
    anchor = 'static void __attribute__((constructor)) bv_register(void)'
    assert emitted.count(anchor) == 1
    emitted = emitted.replace(anchor, probes + '\n' + anchor)
    for native, probe in [('u64_roundtrip', 'fault'), ('create_buffer', 'create'),
                          ('allocate_memory', 'allocate'), ('write_memory_words', 'write')]:
        emitted, count = re.subn(r'(io_eff\(CID_\w+, )bv_' + native + r'(\);)',
                                 r'\1test_upload_' + probe + r'\2', emitted)
        assert count == 1, native
    source.write_text(emitted)
    flags = ['-fsanitize=address,undefined', '-fno-omit-frame-pointer'] if args.sanitize else []
    subprocess.run(['clang', '-std=c11', '-O1', '-g', *flags, str(source), '-lpthread', '-lm', '-ldl', '-o', str(binary)], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
    environment = dict(os.environ, VK_INSTANCE_LAYERS='VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation', VK_LAYER_VALIDATE_SYNC='1',
                       ASAN_OPTIONS='detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1', UBSAN_OPTIONS='halt_on_error=1')
    result = subprocess.run([str(binary)], cwd=ROOT, env=environment, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=60)
    output = result.stdout + result.stderr
    (ROOT / 'build/m7-upload-failures.log').write_text(output)
    print(output, end='', flush=True)
    result.check_returncode()
    assert 'Six real-device geometry allocation/upload failures preserved installed bytes and rows' in output
    assert not any(item in output for item in ('Validation Error', 'SYNC-HAZARD', 'AddressSanitizer', 'runtime error:'))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### vulkan/tools/check_window.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Drive only the spawned Vulkan window through X11 and retain app-only evidence."""
import argparse
import ctypes as C
import os
import contextlib
import select
import signal
import struct
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess
import time

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BUILD = ROOT / 'build'
U = C.c_ulong
I = C.c_int
P = C.c_void_p

class Client(C.Structure):
    _fields_ = [('type', I), ('serial', U), ('send_event', I), ('display', P), ('window', U),
                ('message_type', U), ('format', I), ('data', C.c_long * 5)]

class Input(C.Structure):
    _fields_ = [('type',I),('serial',U),('send_event',I),('display',P),('window',U),('root',U),('subwindow',U),('time',U),('x',I),('y',I),('x_root',I),('y_root',I),('state',C.c_uint),('code',C.c_uint),('same_screen',I)]

class Event(C.Union):
    _fields_ = [('client', Client), ('input', Input), ('pad', C.c_long * 24)]

class X11:
    def __init__(self):
        self.x = C.CDLL('libX11.so.6')
        signatures = {
            'XOpenDisplay': ([C.c_char_p], P), 'XDefaultRootWindow': ([P], U),
            'XQueryTree': ([P,U,C.POINTER(U),C.POINTER(U),C.POINTER(C.POINTER(U)),C.POINTER(C.c_uint)], I),
            'XFetchName': ([P,U,C.POINTER(C.c_char_p)], I), 'XFree': ([P], I),
            'XInternAtom': ([P,C.c_char_p,I], U),
            'XGetWindowProperty': ([P,U,U,C.c_long,C.c_long,I,U,C.POINTER(U),C.POINTER(I),C.POINTER(U),C.POINTER(U),C.POINTER(P)], I),
            'XSetInputFocus': ([P,U,I,U], I), 'XRaiseWindow': ([P,U], I),
            'XResizeWindow': ([P,U,C.c_uint,C.c_uint], I), 'XMapWindow': ([P,U], I),
            'XUnmapWindow': ([P,U], I), 'XFlush': ([P], I), 'XSync': ([P,I], I),
            'XKeysymToKeycode': ([P,U], C.c_ubyte),
            'XWarpPointer': ([P,U,U,I,I,C.c_uint,C.c_uint,I,I], I),
            'XSendEvent': ([P,U,I,C.c_long,C.POINTER(Event)], I), 'XCloseDisplay': ([P], I),
        }
        for name,(args,result) in signatures.items():
            fn = getattr(self.x,name); fn.argtypes=args; fn.restype=result
        self.d = self.x.XOpenDisplay(None)
        if not self.d:
            raise RuntimeError('A real DISPLAY is required for native-window verification')
        self.root = self.x.XDefaultRootWindow(self.d)
        self.pid_atom = self.x.XInternAtom(self.d,b'_NET_WM_PID',0)
        self.owned = None
        self.position=(0,0)

    def children(self, parent):
        root,parent_out,n = U(),U(),C.c_uint(); children=C.POINTER(U)()
        if not self.x.XQueryTree(self.d,parent,C.byref(root),C.byref(parent_out),C.byref(children),C.byref(n)):
            return []
        result = [children[j] for j in range(n.value)]
        if children: self.x.XFree(children)
        return result

    def title(self, window):
        value=C.c_char_p()
        self.x.XFetchName(self.d,window,C.byref(value))
        result=value.value.decode() if value else ''
        if value: self.x.XFree(value)
        return result

    def pid(self, window):
        actual,fmt,n,after,data=U(),I(),U(),U(),P()
        self.x.XGetWindowProperty(self.d,window,self.pid_atom,0,1,0,6,C.byref(actual),C.byref(fmt),C.byref(n),C.byref(after),C.byref(data))
        result = C.cast(data,C.POINTER(U))[0] if data and n.value else 0
        if data: self.x.XFree(data)
        return result

    def find(self, pid):
        stack = self.children(self.root)
        while stack:
            window = stack.pop()
            if self.pid(window)==pid:
                self.owned=window
                return window
            stack.extend(self.children(window))
        return None

    def flush(self): self.x.XSync(self.d,0)
    def focus(self):
        self.x.XRaiseWindow(self.d,self.owned)
        self.x.XSetInputFocus(self.d,self.owned,1,0)
        self.flush()
    def key(self, symbol, down):
        event=Event(); event.input.type=2 if down else 3; event.input.display=self.d
        event.input.window=self.owned; event.input.root=self.root; event.input.same_screen=1
        event.input.code=self.x.XKeysymToKeycode(self.d,symbol)
        self.x.XSendEvent(self.d,self.owned,0,1 if down else 2,C.byref(event)); self.flush()
    def tap(self, symbol):
        self.key(symbol,1); time.sleep(.04); self.key(symbol,0)
    def button(self, button, down):
        event=Event(); event.input.type=4 if down else 5; event.input.display=self.d
        event.input.window=self.owned; event.input.root=self.root; event.input.same_screen=1
        event.input.code=button; event.input.x=self.position[0]; event.input.y=self.position[1]
        self.x.XSendEvent(self.d,self.owned,0,4 if down else 8,C.byref(event)); self.flush()
    def move(self,x,y):
        self.position=(x,y)
        self.x.XWarpPointer(self.d,0,self.owned,0,0,0,0,x,y); self.flush()
    def moves(self,points):
        for x,y in points:
            self.position=(x,y)
            self.x.XWarpPointer(self.d,0,self.owned,0,0,0,0,x,y)
        self.flush()
    def resize(self,w,h):
        self.x.XResizeWindow(self.d,self.owned,w,h); self.flush()
    def map(self, mapped):
        getattr(self.x,'XMapWindow' if mapped else 'XUnmapWindow')(self.d,self.owned); self.flush()
    def close(self):
        event=Event(); event.client.type=33; event.client.display=self.d; event.client.window=self.owned
        event.client.message_type=self.x.XInternAtom(self.d,b'WM_PROTOCOLS',0); event.client.format=32
        event.client.data[0]=self.x.XInternAtom(self.d,b'WM_DELETE_WINDOW',0)
        self.x.XSendEvent(self.d,self.owned,0,0,C.byref(event)); self.flush()


def states(log):
    result=[]
    for line in log.read_text().split('\n')[:-1]:
        if line.startswith('STATE '):
            value={k: int(v) if v.isdigit() else v for k,v in re.findall(r'(\w+)=([^ ]+)',line)}
            if isinstance(value.get('camera'),str) and value['camera'].count(',')==4:
                result.append(value)
    return result


def run_product(binary):
    from PIL import Image
    log=BUILD/'window-product.log'
    env=dict(os.environ,VK_INSTANCE_LAYERS='VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation',VK_LAYER_VALIDATE_SYNC='1')
    x=X11()
    with log.open('w') as output:
        process=subprocess.Popen([str(binary)],cwd=ROOT,stdout=output,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,env=env)
        def capture(name, expected=None, different=None):
            path=BUILD/f'window-product-{name}.png'
            deadline=time.monotonic()+12
            while time.monotonic()<deadline:
                assert process.poll() is None, log.read_text()[-6000:]
                subprocess.run(['import','-window',str(x.owned),str(path)],check=True,timeout=10)
                image=Image.open(path).convert('RGB')
                colors=image.getcolors(image.width*image.height)
                pixels=image.tobytes()
                if colors and len(colors)>3 and (expected is None or pixels==expected) and pixels!=different:
                    return pixels
                time.sleep(.1)
            raise AssertionError(f'{name}: ordinary product pixels did not reach the expected image')
        try:
            deadline=time.monotonic()+12
            while not x.find(process.pid):
                assert process.poll() is None, log.read_text()[-6000:]
                if time.monotonic()>deadline: raise AssertionError('Ordinary product window was not found')
                time.sleep(.05)
            x.focus()
            x.move(292,210); time.sleep(.35)
            intact=capture('intact')
            time.sleep(.2)
            assert capture('hover-settled') == intact
            x.button(1,1); x.button(1,0)
            carved=capture('click-carved',different=intact)
            x.tap(ord('r'))
            capture('reset',expected=intact,different=carved)
            x.map(False); time.sleep(.25)
            assert process.poll() is None, log.read_text()[-6000:]
            x.close(); process.wait(timeout=12)
            text=log.read_text()
            assert process.returncode==0, text[-6000:]
            assert not text.strip(), text
            assert not re.search(r'^(STATE|TIMING) ',text,re.MULTILINE), 'Default product emitted runtime diagnostics'
            assert 'Validation Error' not in text and 'SYNC-HAZARD' not in text, text
            print(f'Ordinary product default quiet, edit pixels, exact reset and suspended WM close passed. Evidence {log.relative_to(ROOT)}',flush=True)
        finally:
            if process.poll() is None:
                process.terminate()
                try: process.wait(timeout=5)
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired: process.kill(); process.wait()
            x.x.XCloseDisplay(x.d)


def run(binary, split=False, suspended_close=False, escape=False):
    from PIL import Image
    suffix = ('split' if split else 'shared') + ('-suspended-close' if suspended_close else '')
    log=BUILD/f'window-{suffix}.log'
    env=dict(os.environ,VK_INSTANCE_LAYERS='VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation',VK_LAYER_VALIDATE_SYNC='1',ASAN_OPTIONS='detect_leaks=0',UBSAN_OPTIONS='halt_on_error=1')
    x=X11()
    with log.open('w') as output:
        process=subprocess.Popen([str(binary),'--cube','--trace',*(['--split'] if split else [])],cwd=ROOT,stdout=output,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,env=env)
        def wait(predicate, message, timeout=12):
            deadline=time.monotonic()+timeout
            while time.monotonic()<deadline:
                values=states(log)
                if values and predicate(values[-1]): return values[-1]
                if process.poll() is not None:
                    raise AssertionError(f'{message}: process exited {process.returncode}\n{log.read_text()[-6000:]}')
                time.sleep(.04)
            raise AssertionError(f'{message}: timed out\n{log.read_text()[-4000:]}')
        def acquisitions():
            complete=log.read_text().rsplit('\n',1)[0]
            values=re.findall(r'COUNTS rows=\d+ allocations=\d+ acquisitions=(\d+)',complete)
            assert values, 'Native dispatch counters are required. Use --build.'
            return int(values[-1])
        def capture(name):
            path=BUILD/f'window-{suffix}-{name}.png'
            deadline=time.monotonic()+5
            while time.monotonic()<deadline:
                subprocess.run(['import','-window',str(x.owned),str(path)],check=True,timeout=10)
                image=Image.open(path).convert('RGB')
                colors=image.getcolors(image.width*image.height)
                if colors and len(colors)>3: return image.tobytes()
                time.sleep(.1)
            raise AssertionError(f'{name}: rendered window has no cube')
        def changed_capture(name, previous):
            deadline=time.monotonic()+8
            while time.monotonic()<deadline:
                pixels=capture(name)
                if pixels!=previous: return pixels
                time.sleep(.1)
            raise AssertionError(f'{name}: camera/model changed without changed window pixels')
        try:
            deadline=time.monotonic()+10
            while not x.find(process.pid):
                if time.monotonic()>deadline: raise AssertionError('Owned application window was not found')
                time.sleep(.05)
            x.focus(); x.button(3,0); x.button(1,0)
            before=wait(lambda s:s['frame']>=3,'Initial presentation')
            acquisitions()
            intact=capture('intact')
            x.key(ord('w'),1); time.sleep(.15); x.key(ord('w'),0)
            flown=wait(lambda s:s['camera']!=before['camera'] and s['frame']>before['frame']+2,'WASD flight')
            assert flown['uploads']==before['uploads'], 'Camera flight rebuilt the mesh'
            wait(lambda s:s['frame']>flown['frame']+2,'Flight presentation')
            flight_pixels=changed_capture('flight',intact)
            x.focus(); x.move(flown['width']//2,flown['height']//2); x.button(3,1)
            wait(lambda s:s['captured']==1,'RMB capture')
            os.kill(process.pid,signal.SIGSTOP)
            try: x.moves([(flown['width']//2+10,flown['height']//2+3),(flown['width']//2+22,flown['height']//2+9)])
            finally: os.kill(process.pid,signal.SIGCONT)
            before_yaw=struct.unpack('<f',struct.pack('<I',int(flown['camera'].split(',')[3])))[0]
            def full_look(state):
                after_yaw=struct.unpack('<f',struct.pack('<I',int(state['camera'].split(',')[3])))[0]
                return abs((before_yaw-after_yaw)-.066)<.00001
            looked=wait(full_look,'Queued pointer motion without loss or double counting')
            x.button(3,0); wait(lambda s:s['captured']==0 and s['frame']>looked['frame']+2,'Capture release')
            look_pixels=changed_capture('look',flight_pixels)
            assert looked['uploads']==before['uploads'], 'Look rebuilt mesh'
            x.move(looked['width']//2,looked['height']//2); x.button(1,1); x.button(1,0)
            carved=wait(lambda s:s['revision']>looked['revision'] and s['uploads']>looked['uploads'],'Click carving')
            wait(lambda s:s['frame']>carved['frame']+2,'Carved presentation')
            click_pixels=changed_capture('click-carved',look_pixels)
            x.tap(ord('r')); reset=wait(lambda s:s['quads']==384 and s['uploads']>carved['uploads'],'Reset')
            wait(lambda s:s['frame']>reset['frame']+2,'Reset presentation')
            reset_pixels=changed_capture('reset',click_pixels)
            assert reset_pixels==look_pixels, 'Reset did not restore the intact cube at the same camera'
            x.tap(32); demo=wait(lambda s:s['quads']==396 and s['uploads']>reset['uploads'],'Demo cut')
            wait(lambda s:s['frame']>demo['frame']+2,'Demo presentation'); changed_capture('demo-carved',reset_pixels)
            for width,height in [(800,450),(450,700),(720,480),(480,720),(800,450)]:
                old=states(log)[-1]; x.resize(width,height)
                resized=wait(lambda s:s['width']==width and s['height']==height and s['generation']>old['generation'] and s['frame']>old['frame']+2,'Non-square recreation')
                x.tap(ord('r')); clean=wait(lambda s:s['quads']==384 and s['uploads']>resized['uploads'],'Resized reset')
                wait(lambda s:s['frame']>clean['frame']+2,'Resized reset presentation')
                clean_pixels=capture(f'{width}x{height}-intact')
                x.move(width//2,height//2); x.button(1,1); x.button(1,0)
                edited=wait(lambda s:s['revision']>clean['revision'] and s['uploads']>clean['uploads'],'Extent-aware picking')
                wait(lambda s:s['frame']>edited['frame']+2,'Resized edit presentation')
                edited_pixels=changed_capture(f'{width}x{height}-carved',clean_pixels)
                x.tap(ord('r')); reset=wait(lambda s:s['quads']==384 and s['uploads']>edited['uploads'],'Resized reset after edit')
                wait(lambda s:s['frame']>reset['frame']+2,'Resized intact presentation')
                assert changed_capture(f'{width}x{height}-reset',edited_pixels)==clean_pixels, 'Resized reset did not restore the intact cube at the same camera'
            capture('resized')
            x.button(3,1); wait(lambda s:s['captured']==1,'Capture before focus loss')
            x.x.XSetInputFocus(x.d,x.root,1,0); x.flush()
            wait(lambda s:s['captured']==0,'Focus loss releases capture')
            x.focus(); x.map(False)
            suspended=wait(lambda s:s['mapped']==0,'Unmap suspension')
            suspended_acquisitions=acquisitions()
            time.sleep(.25); current=states(log)[-1]
            assert acquisitions()==suspended_acquisitions, 'Suspended app continued native acquisition dispatch'
            assert current['frame']<=suspended['frame']+2 and current['generation']==suspended['generation'], 'Suspended app continued acquisition/recreation'
            if not suspended_close:
                x.map(True); x.focus(); restored=wait(lambda s:s['mapped']==1 and s['frame']>current['frame']+2,'Restore')
                x.tap(ord('r')); wait(lambda s:s['quads']==384 and s['uploads']>restored['uploads'],'Input after restore')
                capture('restored')
            if escape: x.key(0xff1b,1)
            else: x.close()
            process.wait(timeout=12)
            text=log.read_text()
            assert process.returncode==0, text[-6000:]
            assert 'Native interactive shutdown rows=0' in text
            counts=[tuple(map(int,pair)) for pair in re.findall(r'COUNTS rows=(\d+) allocations=(\d+)',text)]
            assert counts, "Native allocation counters were not recorded"
            if counts:
                assert 'Native metadata allocations=0' in text
                assert max(rows for rows,allocs in counts)<100
                assert max(allocs for rows,allocs in counts)<300
                assert counts[-1]==(0,0)
            steady=[s['rows'] for s in states(log) if s['generation']>=1 and s['uploads']>=2]
            assert max(steady)-min(steady)<=16, 'Recreation or editing leaked rows'
            assert 'Validation Error' not in text and 'SYNC-HAZARD' not in text, text
            assert 'AddressSanitizer' not in text and 'runtime error:' not in text, text
            queues=re.search(r'Queues (\d+) (\d+)',text)
            assert queues and ((queues[1]!=queues[2]) if split else (queues[1]==queues[2]))
            print(f'Native window {suffix} passed. Evidence {log.relative_to(ROOT)}',flush=True)
        finally:
            if x.owned and process.poll() is None:
                x.button(3,0); x.button(1,0); x.key(ord("w"),0)
            if process.poll() is None:
                process.terminate()
                try: process.wait(timeout=5)
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired: process.kill(); process.wait()
            x.x.XCloseDisplay(x.d)


def deduplicate_native(emitted):
    begin_marker='#ifndef BV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION\n#define BV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION\n'
    end_marker='#endif // BV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION'
    first=emitted.find(begin_marker)
    if first<0: raise ValueError('Native module guard moved')
    start=emitted.find(begin_marker,first+len(begin_marker))
    while start>=0:
        end=emitted.index(end_marker,start)+len(end_marker)
        emitted=emitted[:start]+emitted[end:]
        start=emitted.find(begin_marker,first+len(begin_marker))
    return emitted


def instrument_window(emitted):
    marker='// Linux X11 window effects share the checked registry with native.c.'
    if marker not in emitted:
        return emitted
    begin=emitted.index(marker)
    end=emitted.index('static void __attribute__((constructor)) bv_window_register(void)',begin)
    window=emitted[begin:end]
    window=window.replace('malloc(','bv_test_alloc(').replace('calloc(','bv_test_calloc(').replace('realloc(','bv_test_realloc(')
    window=re.sub(r'\bfree\(','bv_test_free(',window)
    return emitted[:begin]+window+emitted[end:]


def build_window(sanitize=False, compile_timeout=None):
    BUILD.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    source=BUILD/'voxel-interactive-probes.c'
    binary=BUILD/('voxel-interactive-sanitized' if sanitize else 'voxel-interactive-checked')
    subprocess.run(['bend','examples/voxel_interactive.bend','-o',str(source)],cwd=ROOT,check=True,timeout=compile_timeout)
    emitted=deduplicate_native(source.read_text())
    allocator=(ROOT/'../deps/bend-vulkan/tests/probes.c').read_text().split('static unsigned bv_test_barrier_calls;')[0]+'\nstatic uint64_t bv_test_acquisitions;\n'
    begin=emitted.index('// Bend native effect ABI.')
    end=emitted.index('static void __attribute__((constructor)) bv_register(void)',begin)
    native=emitted[begin:end]
    native=native.replace('malloc(', 'bv_test_alloc(').replace('calloc(', 'bv_test_calloc(').replace('realloc(', 'bv_test_realloc(')
    native=re.sub(r'\bfree\(', 'bv_test_free(',native)
    native=native.replace('static BvRow* bv_add(',allocator+'\nstatic BvRow* bv_add(',1)
    acquire_dispatch='uint32_t index=0;result=bv_device_result(owner,device->AcquireNextImageKHR('
    if native.count(acquire_dispatch)!=1: raise ValueError('Acquire dispatch moved')
    native=native.replace(acquire_dispatch,'uint32_t index=0;bv_test_acquisitions++;result=bv_device_result(owner,device->AcquireNextImageKHR(')
    a=native.index('static Term bv_live_objects(');b=native.index('\n}',a)
    check='  printf("COUNTS rows=%u allocations=%u acquisitions=%llu\\n",count,bv_test_live_allocations,(unsigned long long)bv_test_acquisitions);fflush(stdout);\n  if(!count) {assert(bv_test_live_allocations==0);puts("Native metadata allocations=0");fflush(stdout);}\n'
    native=native[:a]+native[a:b].replace('  return io_done(e,count);',check+'  return io_done(e,count);')+native[b:]
    source.write_text(instrument_window(emitted[:begin]+native+emitted[end:]))
    flags=['-fsanitize=address,undefined','-fno-omit-frame-pointer'] if sanitize else []
    subprocess.run(['clang','-std=c11','-O1','-g',*flags,str(source),'-lpthread','-lm','-ldl','-o',str(binary)],check=True,cwd=ROOT,timeout=compile_timeout)
    return binary

@contextlib.contextmanager
def isolated_display(enabled):
    if not enabled:
        yield
        return
    read_fd,write_fd=os.pipe()
    output=(BUILD/'window-xwayland.log').open('w')
    server=subprocess.Popen(['Xwayland','-displayfd',str(write_fd),'-geometry','1024x768','-noreset','-nolisten','tcp'],pass_fds=[write_fd],stdout=output,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    os.close(write_fd)
    previous=os.environ.get('DISPLAY')
    try:
        if not select.select([read_fd],[],[],10)[0]: raise RuntimeError('Isolated Xwayland did not start')
        number=os.read(read_fd,64).decode().strip()
        if not number.isdigit(): raise RuntimeError('Isolated Xwayland returned no display')
        os.environ['DISPLAY']=':'+number
        yield
    finally:
        if previous is None: os.environ.pop('DISPLAY',None)
        else: os.environ['DISPLAY']=previous
        os.close(read_fd)
        server.terminate()
        try: server.wait(timeout=5)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired: server.kill(); server.wait()
        output.close()


def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--binary',type=Path,help='reuse an instrumented binary, or an ordinary binary with --product')
    parser.add_argument('--product',action='store_true',help='build and check the ordinary executable without diagnostic flags')
    parser.add_argument('--build',action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--sanitize',action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--isolated-display',action='store_true',help='run a private rootful Xwayland server on the current Wayland desktop')
    parser.add_argument('--all',action='store_true',help='run shared queues, split queues, and suspended close sequentially')
    parser.add_argument('--split',action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--suspended-close',action='store_true')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    if not args.isolated_display and not os.environ.get('DISPLAY'): raise SystemExit('DISPLAY is required; use tools/verify.py for headless regression')
    BUILD.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    if args.product:
        if args.all or args.split or args.suspended_close or args.sanitize or args.build:
            parser.error('--product requires the ordinary unsanitized executable without acceptance modifiers')
        binary=args.binary.resolve() if args.binary else BUILD/'voxel-interactive'
        if not args.binary:
            subprocess.run(['bend','examples/voxel_interactive.bend','-o',str(binary)],cwd=ROOT,check=True)
    else:
        binary=build_window(args.sanitize) if args.build or args.sanitize or args.binary is None else args.binary.resolve()
    with isolated_display(args.isolated_display):
        if args.product:
            run_product(binary)
        elif args.all:
            run(binary,False,False,escape=True)
            run(binary,True,False)
            run(binary,False,True)
        else:
            run(binary,args.split,args.suspended_close)

if __name__=='__main__': main()
```

### vulkan/tools/geometry_clips.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def cells(rect):
    a, b, c, d = rect
    assert a < b and c < d, f'nonpositive rectangle {rect}'
    return {(u, v) for u in range(a, b) for v in range(c, d)}


def check(path):
    count = 0
    keys = set()
    for line in Path(path).read_text().splitlines():
        if not line.startswith('['):
            continue
        source, blocker, actual = json.loads(line)
        key = tuple(source + blocker)
        assert key not in keys
        keys.add(key)
        remaining = cells(source) - cells(blocker)
        seen = set()
        assert len(actual) <= 4, 'more than four fragments'
        for rectangle in actual:
            covered = cells(rectangle)
            assert not covered & seen, 'duplicate subtraction coverage'
            seen |= covered
        assert seen == remaining, f'wrong difference {source} {blocker}'
        count += 1
    assert count == 10000, f'incomplete 0..4 valid rectangle cross-product {count}'
    print(f'PASS exhaustive {count} rectangle differences, positive fragments, exact coverage and disjointness')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    check(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'build/geometry-clips.jsonl')
```

### vulkan/tools/geometry_oracle.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import array
import hashlib
import json
import math
import struct
import sys
from pathlib import Path

LIMIT = 1 << 20
QUAD_LIMIT = (1 << 30) // 40
ERRORS = {
    'overlap': 'overlapping nonair cuboids at sources 0 and 1',
    'contained': 'overlapping nonair cuboids at sources 0 and 1',
    'duplicate': 'overlapping nonair cuboids at sources 0 and 1',
    'invalid-box': 'invalid nonempty cuboid bounds at source 0',
    'inverted-box': 'invalid nonempty cuboid bounds at source 0',
    'coordinate-over': 'cuboid coordinates exceed 2^20 at source 0',
    'invalid-dense-side': 'dense body must be a balanced side^3 array with a positive power-of-two side <= 1024',
    'invalid-dense-shape': 'dense body must be a balanced side^3 array with a positive power-of-two side <= 1024',
    'zero-width': 'invalid face material, extent or coordinate bounds',
    'extent-over': 'invalid face material, extent or coordinate bounds',
    'extent-wrap': 'invalid face material, extent or coordinate bounds',
}


def need(condition, message):
    if not condition:
        raise AssertionError(message)


def f32(word):
    return struct.unpack('<f', struct.pack('<I', word))[0]


def bits(value):
    return struct.unpack('<I', struct.pack('<f', value))[0]


def sha(data):
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def little_words(words):
    result = array.array('I', words)
    if sys.byteorder != 'little':
        result.byteswap()
    return result.tobytes()


def size_check(q, size):
    vc, ic, vw, iw = q * 4, q * 6, q * 40, q * 6
    vcap = 1 << max(0, (vw - 1).bit_length()) if vw else 1
    icap = 1 << max(0, (iw - 1).bit_length()) if iw else 1
    expected = [q, vc, ic, vw, iw, vcap, icap, vcap.bit_length() - 1, icap.bit_length() - 1]
    need(size['counts'] == expected, f'size counts {size["counts"]} != {expected}')
    for key, value in [('vertex_bytes', vw * 4), ('index_bytes', iw * 4)]:
        need(size[key] == [value & 0xffffffff, value >> 32], f'{key} overflow')
    need(max(vcap, icap) <= 1 << 30, 'array address overflow')
    need(max(vc, ic) <= 0xffffffff, 'draw/index overflow')
    return vw, iw, vcap, icap


def rgb_palette():
    authored = [(0.736340, 0.114340, 173.8965), (0.733411, 0.093103, 66.0436),
                (0.630974, 0.062930, 239.8232), (0.723628, 0.119039, 64.7019),
                (0.860000, 0.018000, 85.0000)]

    def linear(lightness, chroma, hue):
        a, b = chroma * math.cos(math.radians(hue)), chroma * math.sin(math.radians(hue))
        l = (lightness + .3963377774 * a + .2158037573 * b) ** 3
        m = (lightness - .1055613458 * a - .0638541728 * b) ** 3
        s = (lightness - .0894841775 * a - 1.2914855480 * b) ** 3
        return [4.0767416621 * l - 3.3077115913 * m + .2309699292 * s,
                -1.2684380046 * l + 2.6097574011 * m - .3413193965 * s,
                -.0041960863 * l - .7034186147 * m + 1.7076147010 * s]

    def encode(channel):
        value = max(0, min(channel, 1))
        return math.floor(255 * (12.92 * value if value <= .0031308 else 1.055 * value ** (1 / 2.4) - .055))

    result = {}
    for material, (lightness, chroma, hue) in enumerate(authored, 1):
        color = linear(lightness, chroma, hue)
        if not all(0 <= c <= 1 for c in color):
            low, high = 0, chroma
            for _ in range(24):
                middle = (low + high) / 2
                if all(0 <= c <= 1 for c in linear(lightness, middle, hue)):
                    low = middle
                else:
                    high = middle
            color = linear(lightness, low, hue)
        swatches = []
        for amount in (1, .58, .35):
            r, g, b = [encode(c * amount) for c in color]
            swatches.append(r << 16 | g << 8 | b)
        result[str(material)] = swatches
    return result


def packed_check(mesh, palette):
    fs, vs, ix = mesh['faces'], mesh['vertices'], mesh['indices']
    q = len(fs)
    vw, iw, vcap, icap = size_check(q, mesh['size'])
    need(len(vs) == vcap and len(ix) == icap, 'actual array capacity mismatch')
    need(not any(vs[vw:]) and not any(ix[iw:]), 'nonzero padding')
    all_low, all_high = [LIMIT] * 3, [0] * 3
    directions = set()
    for number, f in enumerate(fs):
        kind, x, y, z, direction, material, width, height = f
        anchor = [x, y, z]
        need(kind in (0, 1) and 0 <= direction < 6, 'face tag/direction')
        need(kind == 1 or (width, height) == (1, 1), 'unit face size')
        need(material != 0 and width > 0 and height > 0, 'nonpositive/air patch')
        normal_axis = direction // 2
        tangent_axes = [axis for axis in range(3) if axis != normal_axis]
        u, v = tangent_axes
        high = anchor.copy()
        high[normal_axis] += 1
        high[u] += width
        high[v] += height
        need(all(0 <= lo < hi <= LIMIT for lo, hi in zip(anchor, high)), 'face bound')
        for axis in range(3):
            all_low[axis] = min(all_low[axis], anchor[axis])
            all_high[axis] = max(all_high[axis], high[axis])
        plane = anchor[normal_axis] + direction % 2
        expected_corners = set()
        for du in (0, width):
            for dv in (0, height):
                point = anchor.copy()
                point[normal_axis] = plane
                point[u] += du
                point[v] += dv
                expected_corners.add(tuple(point))
        records = [vs[number * 40 + i * 10:number * 40 + (i + 1) * 10] for i in range(4)]
        points = [tuple(f32(word) for word in r[:3]) for r in records]
        need(set(points) == expected_corners and len(set(points)) == 4, f'corners at {number}')
        need(points[0] == tuple(anchor[a] if a != normal_axis else plane for a in range(3)), 'first corner ordering')
        light = 0 if direction == 5 else 1 if direction < 2 else 2
        rgb = palette[str(material)][light] if str(material) in palette else 0xff00ff
        encoded = ((rgb >> 16) & 255) | (rgb & 0xff00) | ((rgb & 255) << 16) | (direction << 24)
        for record in records:
            need(record[3] == encoded, f'basic RGB/direction at {number}')
            need(record[4] == material, f'full U32 material at {number}')
            need(record[5:10] == [bits(value) for value in anchor + [width, height]], f'flat identity at {number}')
        actual_ix = ix[number * 6:number * 6 + 6]
        need(actual_ix == [number * 4 + i for i in (0, 1, 2, 2, 3, 0)], f'indices at {number}')
        normal = [0, 0, 0]
        normal[normal_axis] = 1 if direction % 2 else -1
        for start in (0, 3):
            a, b, c = [points[i - number * 4] for i in actual_ix[start:start + 3]]
            ab, ac = [b[i] - a[i] for i in range(3)], [c[i] - a[i] for i in range(3)]
            cross = [ab[1] * ac[2] - ab[2] * ac[1], ab[2] * ac[0] - ab[0] * ac[2], ab[0] * ac[1] - ab[1] * ac[0]]
            need(cross == [value * width * height for value in normal], f'outward area at {number}')
        directions.add(direction)
    need(mesh['bounds'] == (all_low + all_high if q else None), 'bounds metadata')
    vertex_bytes, index_bytes = little_words(vs[:vw]), little_words(ix[:iw])
    return {'quads': q, 'vertex_bytes': len(vertex_bytes), 'index_bytes': len(index_bytes),
            'geometry_sha256': sha(vertex_bytes + index_bytes), 'directions': sorted(directions)}


class Occupancy:
    def __init__(self, body):
        self.body = body
        self.sources = {}
        if 'side' in body:
            n = body['side']
            need(n > 0 and n & (n - 1) == 0, 'dense shape')
            need(len(body['cells']) == n ** 3, 'dense cells')
            self.low, self.shape = [0, 0, 0], [n, n, n]
            self.cells = array.array('I', body['cells'])
        else:
            boxes = body['boxes']
            for b in boxes:
                need(all(0 <= b[a] < b[a + 3] <= LIMIT for a in range(3)), 'source bounds')
            solid = [b for b in boxes if b[6]]
            self.low = [min((b[a] for b in solid), default=0) for a in range(3)]
            high = [max((b[a + 3] for b in solid), default=0) for a in range(3)]
            self.shape = [high[a] - self.low[a] for a in range(3)]
            self.cells = array.array('I', [0]) * math.prod(self.shape)
            sx, sy, sz = self.shape
            for rank, b in enumerate(boxes):
                if not b[6]:
                    continue
                lo = [b[a] - self.low[a] for a in range(3)]
                hi = [b[a + 3] - self.low[a] for a in range(3)]
                row = array.array('I', [b[6]]) * (hi[0] - lo[0])
                for z in range(lo[2], hi[2]):
                    for y in range(lo[1], hi[1]):
                        start = (z * sy + y) * sx + lo[0]
                        need(not any(self.cells[start:start + len(row)]), 'source nonair overlap')
                        self.cells[start:start + len(row)] = row
                for direction in range(6):
                    axis = direction // 2
                    plane = b[axis + 3] if direction % 2 else b[axis]
                    self.sources.setdefault((direction, plane), []).append((rank, b))
        self.occupied = sum(bool(m) for m in self.cells)
        self.expected = bytearray(len(self.cells))
        sx, sy, sz = self.shape
        strides = [1, sx, sx * sy]
        for i, mat in enumerate(self.cells):
            if not mat:
                continue
            local = [i % sx, i // sx % sy, i // (sx * sy)]
            mask = 0
            for axis in range(3):
                for positive in (0, 1):
                    direction = axis * 2 + positive
                    neighbor = local[axis] + (1 if positive else -1)
                    if neighbor < 0 or neighbor >= self.shape[axis] or not self.cells[i + (strides[axis] if positive else -strides[axis])]:
                        mask |= 1 << direction
            self.expected[i] = mask

    def index(self, xyz):
        local = [xyz[a] - self.low[a] for a in range(3)]
        need(all(0 <= local[a] < self.shape[a] for a in range(3)), 'emitted face outside source extent')
        return (local[2] * self.shape[1] + local[1]) * self.shape[0] + local[0]

    def coverage(self, mesh):
        seen = bytearray(len(self.cells))
        order = []
        for face in mesh['faces']:
            kind, x, y, z, direction, material, width, height = face
            anchor = [x, y, z]
            axis = direction // 2
            tangents = [a for a in range(3) if a != axis]
            if 'boxes' in self.body:
                candidates = []
                for rank, b in self.sources[(direction, anchor[axis] + direction % 2)]:
                    if b[6] == material and all(b[a] <= anchor[a] and anchor[a] + extent <= b[a + 3] for a, extent in zip(tangents, [width, height])):
                        candidates.append(rank)
```
